dsh-v0.2.2-rc.2v0.2.2-rc.2 Prerelease2026-11-11T11:33:06Z

中文 | English

<h3 id="cn-v0.2.2-rc.2">新增功能</h3>

  • Git Worktree 支持按分支、提交和文件类型审阅改动，并直接处理 GitHub 评审、同步已审阅进度和更新分支。 @LegGasai
  • SubAgent 支持具名与多层协作，AgentTeam 可按会话开启，共用成员与任务面板。 @Dudu-0223, @Yifffan
  • 桌面端支持在设置中切换正式版与开发版更新渠道。 @winewill

体验优化

  • 改善代码审阅时的评论、文件导航与窄屏布局，减少查看大型改动时的卡顿，并放宽大型仓库创建工作树的等待时间。 @LegGasai
  • 改善多层子代理的浏览与任务协调，优先展示任务描述，支持逐层展开和读取失败后重试。 @Dudu-0223
  • 优化左侧栏插件入口的收纳，不常用入口可移入“更多”，重新固定时保持原顺序。 @Yifffan
  • 简化个性化页面安装后的操作，移除“保留”按钮，统一使用撤销与恢复。 @ZiyaZhang
  • 改善自定义主题对通知、卡片、计划和任务等界面颜色的覆盖。 @turtle2099
  • 改善页面缩放、平移及长列表场景下的菜单定位，保持菜单与触发位置对齐；自定义浮层需适配新定位接口。 @Stevring, @LegGasai
  • 改善暗色模式下文件树浮层的显示，使用背景层次区分面板，避免白色投影。 @turtle2099
  • 改善在侧栏查找文件的体验，支持按名称或路径搜索，并可调整排除规则与隐藏文件显示。 @yudshj
  • 改善侧栏长列表的浏览与拖动体验，插件导航和会话列表共用滚动区域，工作区工具栏保持可见。 @Yifffan
  • 优化从会话打开工作目录的操作，统一“在应用中打开”菜单，并改善窄屏使用体验。 @Yifffan
  • 加快文件右键菜单的打开；关联应用查询较慢时，先提供默认应用入口。 @yudshj
  • 向上回看聊天时自动加载更早消息，优先保持阅读位置，并保留手动加载和重试入口。 @imccyu
  • 改善聊天过程的可查看性，可展开工作区指令、技能目录等信息，了解模型收到的上下文。 @turtle2099
  • 改善补丁编辑的回执，折叠状态下也可查看修改的文件，并在成功后直接打开。 @sjw1231
  • 优化会话首页的交互动画。 @ZiyaZhang
  • 改善会话恢复或压缩后的上下文提示，明确当前运行信息与旧信息的替换关系。 @kermanx
  • 改善聊天中代码差异的阅读，显示增删行颜色，并按文件语言高亮。 @mektpoy
  • 改善粘贴图片和文件的体验，未聚焦输入框时也可粘贴到最近使用的可用输入框或主对话。 @std4453
  • 改善暂停、恢复定时任务时的操作反馈，保持按钮焦点与显示稳定，避免重复操作。 @turtle2099
  • 优化目标栏的操作排列与按钮间距，统一为“编辑、清除、暂停或恢复”。 @turtle2099
  • 优化模型请求失败后的重试设置，可在模型设置中选择重试条件、次数和等待时间。 @turtle2099
  • 改善临时请求失败后的恢复操作，可直接点击“继续生成”，无需另写消息。 @LegGasai
  • 改善 API Key 的管理，已保存密钥以掩码显示，支持整体替换或清除，不显示完整旧密钥。 @Magolor
  • 优化多问题检索，单次网页搜索默认支持最多 10 个查询和 10 个来源。 @turtle2099
  • 改善 Agent 终端的可见性与接管体验，命令在侧栏终端运行，用户输入即可接管；允许读取的用户终端内容会进入模型与会话记录。 @LegGasai
  • 优化批量查询文件、任务和资源时的执行效率，更多只读工具可并行运行，结果仍按调用顺序返回。 @turtle2099
  • 改善桌面更新下载时的进度显示，可点击下载百分比或“检查更新”查看状态。 @winewill

问题修复

  • 修复 PR 更新后审阅差异与提交行号不一致的问题，并改善大量评审讨论的统计。 @LegGasai
  • 修复只有 SSH 凭据时无法查看私有 Git 插件详情的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复可选插件加载失败导致整个 Web 页面无法启动的问题；必需插件失败仍会提示。 @imccyu
  • 修复已启用但未成功加载的插件容易被误认为正在运行的问题，明确显示“未运行”及原因。 @turtle1999
  • 修复 PTC 中重复等待子代理消息时，同一条消息反复触发唤醒的问题。 @Dudu-0223
  • 修复浏览特殊文件名或扩展名时，文件树视图崩溃的问题。 @LegGasai
  • 修复“未分组”会话中显示无效“新建会话”按钮的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复复制聊天公式时出现乱码的问题，同一条 Markdown 内容中的公式可复制为 TeX 源码。 @turtle2099
  • 修复查看 Perl 代码时，正则表达式导致后续内容高亮错误的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复聊天更新出错后，历史加载和新消息显示持续卡住的问题。 @Magolor
  • 改善重复创建会话分支时的标题编号，跳过已用编号，减少同名会话。 @turtle2099
  • 修复停止任务或关闭会话时，部分错误导致任务未中止或资源提前释放的问题。 @liukx0205
  • 修复发送消息或准备附件期间重试请求，导致同一消息被重复提交的问题。 @liukx0205
  • 修复中文目标栏混淆“待继续”与“已暂停”状态的问题。 @imccyu
  • 修复桌面端多次启动后，因鉴权信息累积而无法启动的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复 Windows 通过短路径启动命令行时，无法保存欢迎页确认的问题。 @winewill
  • 修复 macOS 窗口拖动与粘贴快捷键问题，以及 Windows 暗色模式启动白闪。 @yxy72, @winewill, @turtle2099
  • 改善 Windows 更新安装期间的反馈，显示安装状态窗口，减少重启过程中的无提示等待。 @winewill
  • 修复移动端 Safari 在展开屏幕或切换视口后，聊天文字被额外放大的问题，保留用户主动缩放。 @Saafo
  • 修复 Linux 存在长期后台进程时，会话加载与新建变慢的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复会话中移除或替换工具后，部分模型拒绝继续请求的问题。 @Tuyixiang
  • 通过 Anthropic Messages 接入时，历史前缀变化后省略失效的签名 thinking，减少压缩后持续请求失败。 @turtle2099
  • 修复部分模型网关错误被误报为“API 密钥无效”的问题，使重试按实际响应状态处理。 @LegGasai
  • 修复在 DSH 用户目录中配置 DeepSeek 端点后无法启动的问题；项目目录中的环境配置仍不能覆盖端点。 @LegGasai
  • 修复连续浏览多页 PDF 时加载越来越慢、快速翻页卡顿的问题。 @turtle2099
  • 修复使用 Inspector 查看较大的 Cordis 树时，快照接近上限可能导致服务退出的问题。 @turtle2099

其他变更

  • 网页搜索默认改用 DeepSeek Search API，搜索与获取可分别选择计费账号或 API Key；旧提供方和自定义配置需按升级指南调整。 @yudshj
  • 归档会话默认清理符合条件的 Git 工作树，保留分支与提交；需保留本地目录时请关闭清理，自定义配置与 Forge 插件需迁移。 @LegGasai
  • AgentTeam 改用统一的具名子代理，中断工具默认关闭；自定义插件与 SDK 集成需迁移，共享会话的进程须同步升级。 @Dudu-0223
  • SDK 可通过配置为新会话默认开启团队协作，恢复已有会话时保留原选择。 @Dudu-0223
  • 允许插件动态注册和移除 MCP 服务，并查询状态、重启或探测连接，无需用户逐项维护服务配置。 @LegGasai
  • 插件可按语言扩展聊天代码块的显示，未匹配或渲染失败时回退为普通代码块。 @imccyu
  • 插件索引可提供 GitHub Star 和 npm 周下载量，默认仍按索引提供的顺序排列。 @turtle1999
  • Creator 的开发指引统一到 dsh-doc，用于定制 DSH 并查阅当前版本资料；引用旧技能名称或路径的扩展需迁移。 @turtle1999
  • 自定义模型提供方支持 Gemini 原生接口，可获取模型列表并设置推理等级。 @turtle2099
  • 模型请求的 User-Agent 增加启动配置标记，便于服务端区分桌面端、Web、命令行与 SDK 请求。 @imccyu
  • 调整账号通知，支持产品公告等消息并随界面语言更新；已有登录保留，自定义账号插件与配置需迁移。 @lsdsjy
  • PTC 按运行时限限制工具调用总量，超限会终止代码并清空保留状态，已完成的操作不会撤销。 @kermanx
  • Session 支持批量追加事件，日志格式不变；自定义追加接口的包装代码需适配批量调用。 @liukx0205
  • 工具失败提供更明确的错误类型与代码，便于定位问题；依赖旧错误分类的插件和 SDK 集成需同步适配。 @sjw1231
  • 调整 Computer Use 的窗口操作、键盘控制和授权，默认不移动系统鼠标；自定义脚本与 helper 需同步升级。 @CreatixChu, @Tuyixiang, @Dudu-0223
  • Office 改用原生工具，Web 与 SDK 均可加载技能；旧包和技能需迁移，文档解析在 DSH 主进程中运行，不受命令行沙箱保护。 @yudshj
  • Windows 的额外目录授权仅供文件工具使用，不扩大终端或 PTC 的访问范围；相关沙箱扩展需迁移。 @Tuyixiang
  • 调整 LiveShare 的多会话分享与插件授权，旧授权需重新配置；PTC 可访问分享目录之外，仅应分享给可信任的人。 @mektpoy
  • npm 安装与桌面端随附对应版本的源码和文档，方便查阅与开发扩展；Python 单文件运行时不包含这些资源。 @turtle1999
  • 升级 Zod 至 4.6.6、Stagehand 至 4.4.0；Stagehand 改为自动发现扩展，旧 extensionId 配置不再生效。 @imccyu

<h3 id="en-v0.2.2-rc.2">New Features</h3>

  • Git Worktree supports reviewing changes by branch, commit, and file category, with GitHub reviews, synchronized viewed-file progress, and branch updates directly in DSH. by @LegGasai
  • SubAgent supports named, multi-level collaboration, while AgentTeam can be enabled per session with a shared member and task panel. by @Dudu-0223, @Yifffan
  • Desktop settings now let you switch between stable and development update channels. by @winewill

Improvements

  • Improve commenting, file navigation, and narrow-screen layouts during code review, reduce lag when viewing large changes, and allow more time to create worktrees in large repositories. by @LegGasai
  • Improve browsing and coordinating nested subagents, prioritizing task descriptions and supporting incremental expansion and retries after failed reads. by @Dudu-0223
  • Improve sidebar plugin organization: move infrequently used entries into More and restore them without changing their original order. by @Yifffan
  • Simplify actions after installing personalized pages, replacing Keep with Undo and Restore. by @ZiyaZhang
  • Improve custom-theme coverage for notifications, cards, plans, tasks, and other interface colors. by @turtle2099
  • Improve menu positioning during page zooming, panning, and browsing long lists, keeping menus aligned with their triggers; custom panels need to adopt the new positioning API. by @Stevring, @LegGasai
  • Improve dark-theme file-tree panels with layered backgrounds instead of white shadows. by @turtle2099
  • Improve file discovery in the sidebar with name and path search, exclusion rules, and hidden-file controls. by @yudshj
  • Improve browsing and dragging in long sidebar lists, with plugin navigation and sessions sharing a scroll area and workspace controls remaining visible. by @Yifffan
  • Streamline opening a session's working directory through a consistent Open in menu, with improved narrow-screen usability. by @Yifffan
  • Open file context menus faster; when application lookup is slow, offer a default-app action first. by @yudshj
  • Automatically load earlier messages when scrolling up through chat, prioritizing reading-position stability while retaining manual loading and retry controls. by @imccyu
  • Make chat activity easier to inspect by expanding workspace instructions, skill catalogs, and other context received by the model. by @turtle2099
  • Improve patch-edit receipts by listing affected files even when collapsed and allowing them to be opened after successful edits. by @sjw1231
  • Improve interaction animations on the session home screen. by @ZiyaZhang
  • Improve context reminders after session resume or compaction by clarifying how current runtime information replaces older information. by @kermanx
  • Improve reading code diffs in chat with added and removed row colors and per-file syntax highlighting. by @mektpoy
  • Improve image and file pasting without a focused composer, routing content to the most recently used available composer or the main conversation. by @std4453
  • Improve feedback when pausing or resuming scheduled tasks, keeping focus and appearance stable while preventing duplicate actions. by @turtle2099
  • Refine goal-bar action order and spacing to Edit, Clear, and Pause or Resume. by @turtle2099
  • Improve model-request retry settings with configurable retry conditions, counts, and wait times in model settings. by @turtle2099
  • Make recovery from transient request failures easier with Continue generating, without requiring another message. by @LegGasai
  • Improve API-key management with masked previews and full replacement or removal, without revealing the complete saved key. by @Magolor
  • Improve multi-question research with up to 10 queries and 10 sources per web-search call by default. by @turtle2099
  • Make Agent terminals visible in the sidebar and let users take over by typing; user-terminal content that agents are permitted to read enters model context and session records. by @LegGasai
  • Improve batch file, task, and resource queries by allowing more read-only tools to run concurrently while returning results in call order. by @turtle2099
  • Improve Desktop update progress displays, with status available from the download percentage or Check for updates. by @winewill

Bug Fixes

  • Fix PR updates causing displayed diffs to use mismatched commit line numbers, and improve counts for large numbers of review threads. by @LegGasai
  • Fix private Git plugin details failing to load for users with SSH-only credentials. by @turtle2099
  • Fix optional plugin failures preventing the entire Web app from starting; required-plugin failures remain visible errors. by @imccyu
  • Fix enabled but unloaded plugins appearing to run, clearly showing Not running and the reason. by @turtle1999
  • Fix repeated waits for subagent messages within PTC waking repeatedly for the same message. by @Dudu-0223
  • Fix file-tree crashes when browsing special filenames or extensions. by @LegGasai
  • Fix a nonfunctional New Session button appearing in the Ungrouped session header. by @turtle2099
  • Fix garbled formula copying in chat, copying formulas within a single Markdown document as TeX source. by @turtle2099
  • Fix Perl regular expressions incorrectly coloring subsequent code. by @turtle2099
  • Fix history loading and new-message displays remaining stuck after a chat-update error. by @Magolor
  • Improve title numbering when repeatedly branching a session, skipping used numbers to reduce duplicate names. by @turtle2099
  • Fix some errors during task cancellation or session shutdown leaving work running or releasing resources prematurely. by @liukx0205
  • Fix retried requests during message submission or attachment preparation submitting the same message more than once. by @liukx0205
  • Fix the Chinese goal bar confusing goals awaiting continuation with paused goals. by @imccyu
  • Fix accumulated authentication data preventing Desktop startup after repeated launches. by @turtle2099
  • Fix welcome-screen confirmation failing to save when the Windows CLI is launched through a short path. by @winewill
  • Fix macOS window dragging and paste shortcuts, and white flashes when starting Windows in dark mode. by @yxy72, @winewill, @turtle2099
  • Improve feedback during Windows update installation with an installation-status window, reducing unexplained waiting during restarts. by @winewill
  • Fix mobile Safari enlarging chat text beyond the configured font size when unfolding or changing the viewport, while preserving manual page zoom. by @Saafo
  • Fix long-running background processes slowing session loading and creation on Linux. by @turtle2099
  • Fix some models rejecting further requests after tools are removed or replaced in a session. by @Tuyixiang
  • For Anthropic Messages connections, omit invalid signed thinking after history-prefix changes to reduce persistent request failures after compaction. by @turtle2099
  • Fix some model-gateway failures being misreported as invalid API keys, allowing retries to follow the actual response status. by @LegGasai
  • Fix startup failure after configuring DeepSeek endpoints in the DSH user directory; project environment settings still cannot override these endpoints. by @LegGasai
  • Fix progressively slower loading and rapid-page-navigation stalls when browsing multi-page PDFs. by @turtle2099
  • Fix inspecting large Cordis trees potentially terminating the service when snapshots approach the size limit. by @turtle2099

Chores

  • Web search now defaults to DeepSeek Search API, with separate account or API-key billing choices for Search and Fetch; migrate older providers and custom settings using the upgrade guide. by @yudshj
  • Archiving sessions cleans up eligible Git worktrees by default while preserving branches and commits; disable cleanup to retain local directories, and migrate custom configuration and Forge plugins. by @LegGasai
  • AgentTeam uses unified named subagents, with the interrupt tool disabled by default; migrate custom plugins and SDK integrations and upgrade all processes sharing session storage together. by @Dudu-0223
  • SDK configuration can enable Team collaboration by default for new sessions while preserving the saved choice when resuming existing sessions. by @Dudu-0223
  • Plugins can dynamically register and remove MCP servers, query their status, restart them, and probe connections without requiring users to maintain each server configuration manually. by @LegGasai
  • Plugins can extend chat code-block rendering by language, falling back to ordinary code blocks when no renderer matches or rendering fails. by @imccyu
  • Plugin indexes can provide GitHub stars and weekly npm downloads while keeping the order supplied by the index as the default. by @turtle1999
  • Creator guidance consolidates into dsh-doc for customizing DSH and consulting current-version resources; extensions referencing old skill names or paths need migration. by @turtle1999
  • Custom model providers support the native Gemini API, including model discovery and reasoning-level settings. by @turtle2099
  • Model-request User-Agent headers include the active profile, helping servers distinguish Desktop, Web, CLI, and SDK requests. by @imccyu
  • Account notifications support announcements and refresh with the interface language; existing sign-ins are retained, while custom account plugins and configuration need migration. by @lsdsjy
  • PTC limits total tool calls according to its runtime budget; exceeding the limit terminates the code and clears retained state without undoing completed operations. by @kermanx
  • Session supports batched event appends without changing the log format; custom append wrappers need to support batch calls. by @liukx0205
  • Tool failures provide clearer error types and codes for diagnosis; plugins and SDK integrations relying on older classifications need updating. by @sjw1231
  • Adjust Computer Use window operations, keyboard control, and authorization, keeping the system pointer still by default; upgrade custom scripts and helpers together. by @CreatixChu, @Tuyixiang, @Dudu-0223
  • Office uses native tools, with skills available in Web and SDK sessions; migrate old packages and skills. Document parsing runs in the DSH host process outside the shell sandbox. by @yudshj
  • Windows extra-directory grants apply only to file tools, not terminal or PTC access; related sandbox extensions need migration. by @Tuyixiang
  • Adjust LiveShare multi-session sharing and plugin authorization; reconfigure old grants. PTC can access files outside the shared directory, so share only with trusted people. by @mektpoy
  • npm installations and Desktop include matching source code and documentation for reference and extension development; Python single-file runtimes exclude these resources. by @turtle1999
  • Upgrade Zod to 4.6.6 and Stagehand to 4.4.0; Stagehand automatically discovers extensions, and the old extensionId setting no longer takes effect. by @imccyu

v0.5.4.1 Stable2026-11-11T03:13:36Z

中文

修复与改进

  • 资源 write：修正文件处理与父目录刷新的衔接，保留每个文件自身的摘要和索引工作；父目录独立刷新，不替代尚未完成的文件索引。wait=true 等待文件自身的处理完成，不等待父目录 L0/L1 和目录索引刷新，请求参数不变。#7383
  • 目录摘要：待刷新期间复用已有文件摘要；恢复 semantic.overview_sample_limit 默认 32 项的稳定采样，文件自身仍完整建立索引。#7380、#7387
  • 存储稳定性：修复 stat 按记录 ID 查询的兼容性、向量 Collection 生命周期，以及多写元数据不完整时的启动问题。#7377、#7355、#7381
  • Connector 与流式响应：支持带作用域的 OAuth 引用，原生导入保留 Connector Watch；修复 Gateway 流式错误的结束标记。#7259、#7364、#7010

其他更新

新增多写 backup 动态切换、账户管理的 global 系统 Skill 目录、火山引擎浏览器授权配置，并更新 Console、FileBrowser、Wiki 和 Agent 体验界面。详见完整变更。

升级说明

pip install --upgrade 'openviking==0.5.4.1'
  • 目录 stat / ls 的 count 现在只统计可见的 L2 记录，不再把 L0/L1 摘要记录计入。#7264
  • OIDC 的非法账户或用户标识会被拒绝，不再通过字符替换映射为其他身份。#7116
  • 本次只发布主包 openviking；独立 Python SDK、CLI 和 React 包保持各自的发布版本。源码集成 React 模块的项目需关注本范围内标记为 breaking 的组件接口变更。

English

Fixes and improvements

  • Resource write: Correct the coordination between file processing and parent-directory refresh. Each file retains its own summary and indexing work, while parent directories refresh independently without superseding pending file indexing. wait=true waits for the file's own processing, not parent L0/L1 summaries or directory indexing. Request parameters are unchanged. #7383
  • Directory summaries: Reuse existing file summaries during pending refreshes and restore stable sampling through semantic.overview_sample_limit, with a default of 32 entries. Each file still receives its own index. #7380, #7387
  • Storage stability: Fix stat compatibility for record IDs, vector Collection lifecycle handling, and startup with incomplete multiwrite metadata. #7377, #7355, #7381
  • Connectors and streaming: Support scoped OAuth references, retain Connector Watches on native imports, and preserve Gateway stream error terminators. #7259, #7364, #7010

Other updates

Add dynamic multiwrite backup switching, account-managed global system Skill directories and VolcEngine browser authorization setup, and update Console, FileBrowser, Wiki and Agent interfaces. See the full changelog.

Upgrade notes

Use pip install --upgrade 'openviking==0.5.4.1'.

  • Directory count in stat / ls now counts visible L2 records only, excluding L0/L1 summary records. #7264
  • Invalid OIDC account or user identifiers are rejected instead of being mapped to another identity through character replacement. #7116
  • This publishes the main openviking package only. Standalone Python SDK, CLI and React packages retain their independent release versions. Source integrations of the React modules should review the component API changes marked as breaking in this range.

首次贡献者 / New contributors

感谢 / Thanks to zhouliangyi-dance (#7274), zuoanCo (#6905), and AtaraxiaZ (#7077).

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 5 releases · 3 stable · 2 prerelease
v0.9.33 Stable2026-11-11T07:00:23Z

HyperFrames v0.9.33

Released on 2026-11-11.

Clips that start partway into a voice recording now keep their sound to the end, even when the WAV file misreports its own byte rate. Variable-bound media also renders at the right length and loudness.

Fixes

  • Engine: A clip that starts partway into a WAV whose header gives the wrong byte rate now plays the right sound to its end, instead of going quiet early or staying silent (7ae1ae649, #7541).
  • Share: When you reach the hourly limit on review versions, the message now explains that limit (8a15d9fee, #7539).
  • Projects: A composition can only look up files inside its own project folder (5a88c9938, #7534).
  • Capture: A wrong captioning region or model name is reported before captioning starts (bcc1437d7, #7533).
  • Producer: A video or sound set through a variable now renders at its clip's length and loudness (789d28121, #7529).

Docs & Examples

  • Variables: Say what can be a variable, including a video or sound per account (28d69d9a9, #7530).

Internal

  • CLI: Give each test file its own home so renders never wait on another file's lock (2b86c49dc, #7526).

v0.9.32 Stable2026-11-11T02:52:14Z

HyperFrames v0.9.32

Released on 2026-11-11.

Long films that are slow to open now render instead of failing before their first frame. The CLI, Studio and the renderer also stop a project folder from choosing what they run or serve.

Features

  • SDR MP4 renders can keep full colour detail with --chroma-subsampling 444, for masters that will be re-encoded; the default is unchanged (1a9fb8acb, #5820).
  • Producer: The render stream's complete event names the producer version that rendered it (7afa05a78, #7515).

Fixes

  • Share: Guide recovery when an export replay is refused (941049a84, #7531).
  • Studio: The composition hover preview runs walled off from Studio's own page, so a composition's scripts cannot reach into Studio (0c229a26c, #7522).
  • Studio: Pressing Enter or Escape while an input method is still composing text no longer commits it, and Escape then leaving a text field no longer saves the cancelled edit (ff285d3f2, #6011).
  • Share: A share or export killed while taking its lock no longer leaves a lock that later attempts cannot clear, on file systems without hard links (b37e4b026, #7516).
  • Producer: Long films that are slow to open no longer fail before their first frame: the wait for the first frame now follows how long the browser took to start, up to 15 minutes (7ff8ece46, #7517).
  • Lint: Flag a script that calls gsap before GSAP loads, and say so when a render fails on it (124591a9d, #7514).
  • Producer,engine: Harden render paths, render slots and local listeners (60fb9d2c5, #7508).
  • Studio Server: Harden preview paths, cache folders and local servers (b8bb49190, #7510).
  • CLI: Keep project folders from choosing the programs and config the CLI runs (84c7d02a1, #7511).
  • CLI: Harden lambda destroy, snapshot media, upgrade and project file serving (c96ff540c, #7509).

Performance

  • Docs: Load showcase film tiles only when they are near the screen (fd0c2337c, #7507).

Docs & Examples

  • List the hub's /blog, /vs and /tools sitemaps in robots.txt (499a17e67, #7527).
  • README: Show the GitHub rank and skills downloads badges (717d3d02c, #7519).

Catalog

  • Harden docs snippets, SVG sanitizer, lint regexes, GSAP writers and registry pins (24508ce2d, #7512).

Internal

  • Inspector: Decode file URLs in HTTP contract fixtures (e7a323ade, #6010).

v0.9.31 Stable2026-11-10T20:22:12Z

HyperFrames v0.9.31

Released on 2026-11-10.

In Safari, long videos no longer load the whole file at once, and playing clips keep their source. The Studio dev server now checks the Host on its API routes, so a tunnel or remote setup may need one setting.

Fixes

  • Core: In Safari, a small video file keeps its preload, and a clip that is playing keeps its source (cda84222a, #7498).
  • Studio: The Studio dev server now checks the Host header on its API routes too, as the preview server does since 0.9.30. A tunnel, Codespaces or remote dev host that worked before can now get a 403: name the host in HYPERFRAMES_PREVIEW_ALLOWED_HOSTS (comma separated), which now also reaches the dev server's own allowed hosts (b0835262a, #7493).
  • Studio Server: Thumbnail links take their origin from the server, and previews from other sites open sandboxed (983e865ad, #7492).
  • Producer: A render that loads the default GSAP file from the CDN uses the copy the CLI already ships, so it works with no network (5c5799975, #7388).
  • CLI: The message about another install shadowing this one only names an install its package manager can move (b28bd2b34, #7499).
  • Studio: Messages that count clips or keyframes say 1 clip, not 1 clips (ec30de062, #7496).
  • Studio Server: When a file is still changing, a claim waits for its first copy, so the edit can still be undone (248f56fba, #7497).

Performance

  • Core: In Safari, the clips of a long video no longer load the whole file at once (fecde9992, #7436).

Docs & Examples

  • Docs: The adopters list adds DoneCut (0b531bc7a, #5638).

Internal

  • Release: Hardened release build.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v15.17.0 Stable2026-11-11T06:13:38Z

New: claude-mem memory for eve agents

  • claude-mem/eve is a new export of the claude-mem npm package. It is a memory provider for eve, Vercel's framework for durable backend agents. An eve agent can now recall what you did in Claude Code, Codex and your other claude-mem clients, using your cmem.ai memory (key from cmem.ai/pro/key) (#5863).
    • Recall: one search before every turn. No inference spend. Matches reach the model as escaped data, never as instructions.
    • Tools: <slot>__search to look further back and <slot>__remember to save a note. Both are bound to the slot's scope.
    • Separate memory per caller: each eve scope gets its own claude-mem project by default. searchAllProjects reads the whole account and is for single-owner agents only.
    • No automatic capture: eve can replay a capture step, and that would save the same turn twice, so saves only happen when the model calls remember.
    • Failures stay visible: if cmem.ai can't be reached, the turn still runs, the failure is logged, and the model is told memory is unavailable.
    • Requires eve 0.84.0 or later and Node.js 24 or later. eve and zod are optional peer dependencies, so nothing changes for existing installs.
  • One-click eve personal agent template at integrations/eve/template: password-protected web chat with your claude-mem memory. Deploy with Vercel and enter two values, EVE_CHAT_PASSWORD and CLAUDE_MEM_API_KEY. iMessage is an eve add-on (npx eve add channel/linq or channel/photon); only texts from CLAUDE_MEM_OWNER_PHONE get your memory.

Quick start for an existing eve agent:

npm install claude-mem

// agent/memory/claude-mem.ts
import { claudeMem } from "claude-mem/eve";
import { defineMemory } from "eve/memory";
import { byPrincipal } from "eve/memory/scope";

export default defineMemory({
  description: "Recall and save durable context for the current user.",
  provider: claudeMem({ apiKey: process.env.CLAUDE_MEM_API_KEY }),
  scope: byPrincipal,
});

Docs: https://docs.claude-mem.ai/eve-integration

No changes to the Claude Code plugin's capture, worker or hooks in this release.

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.16.1...v15.17.0

v15.16.1 Stable2026-11-11T04:58:34Z

Critical fix

  • Manual saves work again in projects with older manual sessions. In v15.16.0, save_memory (and POST /api/memory/save) returned HTTP 500 with UNIQUE constraint failed: sdk_sessions.memory_session_id in any project whose manual session was created before 15.16.0 with an older session ID shape. Every save in that project failed. claude-mem now finds and reuses that older session instead of trying to create a duplicate. Deleted sessions still get a fresh ID, as in 15.16.0 (#5842).

If you are on 15.16.0, update to 15.16.1.

New (both off by default)

  • Opt out of bundled skills: list skill names in CLAUDE_MEM_SKILLS_DISABLED in ~/.claude-mem/settings.json, as a comma-separated string or a JSON array (for example "make-plan,do,smart-explore"). The list is re-applied after installs, repairs and marketplace updates; new skills stay enabled unless you name them (#5837, thanks @liuyinchu, closes #5132).
  • Line anchors in file context: set CLAUDE_MEM_LINE_ANCHORS_ENABLED to "true" and past observations about a file point at the line where that code is now (@L123), or say (code changed since) when it moved away. Nothing is guessed. With the setting off, nothing is recorded and the hook output is unchanged (#5841, thanks @devkancheti4-design, closes #4943).

Fixes

  • OpenClaw: the installer works on OpenClaw 2026.11.2 again, and current OpenClaw records prompts, injects context and summarizes sessions without the removed before_agent_start hook. Older OpenClaw versions behave as before (#5840, fixes #5838, reported by @JesseFranzen).
  • Warp uninstall: npx claude-mem uninstall now removes the claude-mem MCP server that install --ide warp adds to ~/.warp/.mcp.json, instead of leaving it pointed at the deleted plugin (#5839, thanks @tanishqbaweja).
  • Telemetry IDs: recording a consent decision (installer prompt, telemetry enable, telemetry disable) keeps the saved anonymous ID, so one install no longer shows up as two. The telemetry enable disclosure now lists the events actually sent (#5836, thanks @wiesenthal).

v15.16.0 Stable2026-11-11T00:34:23Z

New

  • My Work refresh on your API provider (opt-in): set CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_AGENT=api to run the My Work refresh on the provider you already selected (openrouter, openai-compatible, or the legacy cmem spelling). Installs with no logged-in Claude Code, Codex or Gemini CLI (for example OpenCode or DSH hosts) can now use My Work. With an API provider selected and the setting empty, the refresh no longer falls back to a logged-in Claude Code: no agent runs and nothing is spent until you choose one (#5674, thanks @bulanovk).
  • GitHub MCP pull requests in My Work: pull requests created through GitHub's official MCP create_pull_request tool now show up as My Work products. Only completed results whose URL matches the requested repository are accepted (#5809, thanks @Afloat16).
  • UberLLM preset: uberllm is now a preset of the openai-compatible provider (https://api.uberllm.dev/v1, default model deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash) (#5706, thanks @PSA-Team-source).
  • Tunable live handoff timeout: CLAUDE_MEM_HOOK_HANDOFF_TIMEOUT_MS (default 250) sets the budget for the single live handoff from a hook to the worker, and a missed handoff is now logged at WARN instead of DEBUG (#5831, thanks @mohitdixit02).

Notable fixes

  • Plugin cache cleanup: the worker now prunes old version folders that Claude Code's marketplace updates leave in ~/.claude/plugins/cache/thedotmack/claude-mem/ (one report: 21 versions, about 16 GB). It keeps the running copy and the registered version, and sweeps after boot settles and then hourly (#5685, thanks @MaxWang007).
  • Windows antivirus noise: install, update and launcher rebuilds no longer start a hidden PowerShell when the launcher folder is already on PATH, the pattern Kaspersky flagged (#5835, reported by @Ali-Sabri-0).
  • My Work language: when CLAUDE_MEM_WORK_VIEW_TEXT_LANGUAGE is empty, every refresh step now writes in the system locale's language, so one run no longer mixes languages (#5669, thanks @0xABCD01).
  • My Work idle cost: idle polling no longer rescans every synced row, and the live overview, focus-area merging and heavy panels do less work per update (#5834).
  • Backups: a declined backup run returns a failure, saved backups are reported separately from retention errors, backup intervals beyond the timer limit are honored, and restore rejects stamped snapshots missing worker tables (#5750, #5781, #5784, #5684, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Shell setup: the launcher PATH block and the legacy uninstall alias are written atomically, Cowork stages spooled events under $HOME when the host sets it, and Hermes keeps permanent HTTP client errors out of its circuit breaker (#5680, #5679, #5677, #5678, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • OpenCode: the bash tool is reported as Bash, so SKIP_BASH_PATTERNS applies (#5668, thanks @ryrenz).
  • Sync: a failed Chroma cleanup after a delete is retried (#5673, thanks @Zhong-fan).
  • SessionStart: the cache read and compose steps share one body budget, so a cached body is served only when it fits (#5686, thanks @Varshith-Kali).

More fixes

About 130 smaller fixes from @Afloat16 across capture, sync, search, context, import, transcripts, media, the server, and the Codex, OpenCode, OpenClaw, Hermes, Grok, Kimi, Gemini, Pi, Goose, Notion and Telegram integrations. Highlights:

  • Search and context: literal project keys and file paths are preserved through recall, merges and caching; Unicode and combining marks survive query words and snippet limits; cached context expires after restores and row replacements; the largest fitting timeline prefix is kept.
  • Sync and memory: session titles commit with their outbox mutation and get receiver redaction; manual saves and new prompts work after a session deletion; note revisions survive title dedup.
  • Transcripts and import: multipart Codex messages, paired patch results and zstd frame seams are read correctly; reasoning stays out of final answers; OpenCode, Gemini and restored summaries import without duplicates or lost prompts.
  • Server and providers: API key derivation stays off the request loop; cancellations propagate through retries and compression; split token usage is metered on compatible replies; memory content is sanitized before storage.
  • Media: image ordinals, Read evidence across path spellings, and advertised image dimension bounds are preserved; inline allowance is charged only for accepted images.

Also from @Kirat0201: file history follows whole project merges, interactive timeline search honors project lists, and unified search keeps literal filePath values (#5789, #5787, #5785).

v15.15.2 Stable2026-11-10T19:30:00Z

Critical fix

  • Antigravity no longer denies every tool call after an upgrade from 14.x (#5670): an in-place upgrade from an older install left a PreToolUse hook (and a PreInvocation context hook) in ~/.gemini/config/hooks.json. agy kept firing it before every tool call, the current adapter answered with no decision, and agy denied every call until you re-ran install. Now the Antigravity installer finds those stale entries and migrates the file in place with the same merge install performs. It writes only when something is stale, never throws, and leaves a corrupt file untouched. The worker runs this heal on startup and on Antigravity hook calls, so updating is enough. Thanks to @jiezhiyong for the report (#5665) and to @0xABCD01 for the fix.

Fixes

  • Antigravity: a model call that only called tools no longer becomes an "AntigravityProvider returned Completed" memory. Only a step with a real reply is recorded, so the observer stops writing false memories like "stuck in a retry loop" (#5667, thanks @ryrenz; reported in #5665 by @jiezhiyong).
  • Redaction: with CLAUDE_MEM_REDACT_ENABLED=true, server capture now applies the configured redactor before the capture leaves your machine, and to session and event metadata before it is stored. Before, an untagged credential in an opening prompt or tool payload could be sent over HTTP unredacted (#5774, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Doctor: claude-mem doctor now checks the macOS launcher's code signature. A launcher macOS would kill gets a clear hint (bun upgrade first when Bun is below 1.4.1, otherwise npx claude-mem install rebuilds it); a launcher that still runs only warns (#5657, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • cmem-pro 0.2.1: the Cowork plugin version is bumped so installed copies pick up the redaction and failed-capture fixes made after 0.2.0.

New

  • Turkish My Work: the My Work page is now available in Turkish (#5656, thanks @Kirat0201).

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-11T05:17:50Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2081+g5a53f3bb9e
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1793383622

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v5.1.69 Stable2026-11-11T03:57:40Z

OmO runs on senpi 2026.11.10-2. A client can read and edit the workspace's instruction files over RPC through read_project_file / write_project_file (senpi#6307), a built session can be parked and handed out through claim_session (senpi#6350), the Fable 5.2 prompt preset gains the codebase-ownership sentence (senpi#6360), generate_image is eval-only like bash (senpi#6272), eval cells reach every eval-only tool through tool.<name>(...) and the agent()/output() helpers (senpi#6276), and the EQ goldens carry the new prompt (senpi#6355). (#13054)

An agent that answered a message from another agent can keep answering it in the turns the engine starts on its own, with no prompt. The reply allowance a consumed delivery grants (a reply to that sender by id or stated name, approved from the delivery alone) now lasts for the work the delivery started: the consuming turn and every later turn the engine starts by itself - an open-todo reminder, a goal continuation, a monitor wake. A settle alone no longer ends it; it ends when a new message starts work after that answer: a person's message, or any delivery, whose own sender the new group then holds (a delivery that joins running work adds its sender). A reply in a covered turn continues the delivery's chain at the next hop, so the chain limit still closes it. (#13042, part of #12996) (#13044)

omo remote send now goes through the same delivery pipeline as every other send, and --steer reads the session once. A person's message to a session on another device enters createDeliveryPipeline with the relay address book's reference for the <device>/<session> address, so the CLI no longer builds its own transport call; the relay transport reads the running turn once and steers it, where the CLI used to read it a second time first. A --steer to a session that is running nothing still sends nothing. The unused second prompt implementation (promptRemote) and its argument check are removed. Which sender kinds each device admits is unchanged. (#13038)

A message to a session on this device that waits on a question now says so, as one to another device does. The send is still queued, and its delivery now carries waiting_on: { kind: "approval", device }. device is this install's account device, and is absent when the install has no device identity. (#13035)

Every message one session sends another now names the device it was sent from, on this device and across devices alike, and a message from another device counts as the cause of the receiving run's next send the same way a local one does. When no device id can be read (no device service, or it does not answer), the message still goes and records why it names no device. (#13034, part of #13004)

A message sent or handed off to a session on another device now reports its delivery_id, effective_mode and endpoint, as one sent within this device does. The answer came from the account's session listing and dropped those fields. It now passes the relay transport's own delivery facts through. The endpoint is null because the sender knows no endpoint on that device. (#13033)

A reply to an agent on another device now reaches it by the id in the message header or by its name, with no prompt on the same account. One resolver now finds a thread across this device's threads and the account's other devices: an exact durable id, then an account alias, then the name ladder judged over every entry (two devices sharing a name are an ambiguous_target naming both devices, never a silent pick). thread_send, thread_handoff, the control tools, thread_create (open or fork) and the omo thread CLI all use it. A reply to the sender of a delivery the run took in is approved from that delivery alone - by the sender's durable id or the name it stated - and must resolve to that sender, else it is refused as reply_target_mismatch without sending. The id shown for a thread on another device is its durable id; the relay listing carries it when the device publishes one. The omo remote verbs still dispatch by the id you type as the device's session key, so pass the id the device lists under until that path moves onto the shared resolver. (#13000)

A send to an agent on another device now reports the same facts as one on this device. thread_send and thread_handoff answer delivery_id and effective_mode for a target on another device too, so a caller reads the delivery's id and the lane it took the same way whichever device holds the thread; message_seq and endpoint stay the facts of a gateway row on this device, and device names another one. The reply allowance (a run answering the agent whose delivery it took in, by that agent's id or stated name, with no prompt) is proved identical for a same-device and a cross-device delivery, and one parity table now runs every send assertion against both legs, so a rule that reads the transport fails the build instead of reaching a user. (#13006, on top of #13000)

A task child no longer starts against a senpi engine that refused its process-wide redaction registry; the start fails with SenpiEngineRestartRequiredError, which says to restart omo. The engine refuses to load next to a registry slot it did not install, and that refusal lasts for the whole process, so a restart is the only remedy. (#12991)

omo now installs the signed device service 0.1.27 instead of 0.1.3. The device service pin had stayed at 0.1.3 across every device-service release since 0.1.3, and an installed omo only replaces a service older than its pin, so no machine received a newer one. (#12981)

The thread_* tools are now eval-only, like bash: when an eval tool is present they are no longer in the model's direct tool list and are called as tool.thread_*() inside an eval cell. Behaviour, permissions and results are unchanged, and without eval they stay ordinary direct tools. (#12907, closes #12903)

A long-running engine keeps reclaiming crashed one-shot memory runs. The memory component's cleanup of abandoned one-shot runs, which runs once per engine process outside session opens, now repeats once a day in an engine that stays up, such as the desktop's or a daemon host's, instead of waiting for a restart. (#12899)

Opening a session no longer re-sweeps the memory root. The memory component's cleanup of abandoned one-shot runs now runs once per engine process, shortly after the first open, instead of inside every session open. A session that ends before that cleanup starts hands it to the next open, so crashed runs are still reclaimed. (#12896)

Fixed

  • The senpi barrel load-failure test imported the barrel by a pathname-derived /D:/... specifier, which failed on Windows and turned dev red (#13068). It now imports the barrel by file URL. (#13068)
  • Computer use reports a click or typed text that went out as window events with its real input route, and judges whether input landed only for multi-event input such as drags, so a single click whose window then closes is reported delivered. (#13055)
  • The actionability check no longer refuses press on a Linux button that lists only another action such as click, since the backend presses its first action. (#13055)
  • Computer use flags text typed into a secure field of a background window, and reports how much text was delivered when the user intervenes mid-send. (#13055)
  • On macOS, key priming now bounds and classifies its focus reads, so a slow or failing focus read no longer stalls background typing into a multi-window app. (#13055)
  • Computer use keeps holding the machine-wide interaction lock while it hands key focus back after background typing, so another session can no longer type in between. (#13055)
  • A steer sent to a session on another device that was held behind that session's open draft, and whose turn ended before the draft was released, was answered queued and then lost without a word (#12927). The send now records that refusal: a retry of the same send under the same idempotency_key answers turn_conflict, naming the delivery, instead of queued. (#12935)

Changed

  • Computer use now lets the user keep typing and clicking while it works by default (computer.intervention: "continue"); set it to "pause" to have input wait for the user and stop with UserIntervened. (#13055)
  • While computer use waits for an accessibility target to become actionable, other computer-use sessions of the same user can keep sending input instead of being refused InputBusy; the target is checked once more, without waiting, right before the action. (#13055)
  • Desktop engine: the stop-marker policy is pinned to the XPC-only path and reported by omo doctor. (#12831)

Added

  • Computer use waits until an accessibility target is actionable before it acts, lists the sheets, windows and menus an action opened, suggests the next call when it refuses, and refuses cmd+q, alt+f4 and win+l unless the key chord is forced. (#13055)

  • Wayland (Hyprland): window-targeted pointer input through libei behind a windowInput capability probe (no_eis, hyprland_ipc_missing reasons). (#12831)

  • Wayland (KWin): window-targeted pointer input through the same libei path, and window captures placed on the KWin frame rectangle so coordinates round-trip between capture and click. (#12831)

  • senpi-desktop-sdk: the typed facade package for computer-use v2 consumers (connect, Computer / Window / Element / Frame, reports and verification, typed errors), with a pinned facade manifest and a package-boundaries test. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: skeleton observation (logical depth 3), the web-wrapper rule, and root: <ref> drill with sub-generations (object mode only). (#12831)

  • senpi-desktop-sdk: the spawn transport with the in-session refusal (spawn_inside_session); the service run facade routes windows, displays and capabilities through the SDK. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine host: features-gated session.open options (intervention, macosKeyPriming, trace, reportMode) sent only when the engine advertises them; ActionReport typed exec results with the 7 valid disposition pairs; reserved settings computer.run_sandbox, intervention, macos_key_priming, trace (defaults unchanged); error codes COMPUTER_AMBIGUOUS_TARGET, COMPUTER_CAPTURE_CONSENT_REQUIRED, COMPUTER_PROTECTED_PROCESS with data.reason hints. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: late replies after a stop are quarantined (Suspended, disposition unknown / unknown, audited on stderr); macOS helpers (overlay, keymap, canary) gain parent-death containment. (#12831)

  • Desktop service: a fresh-process permission re-probe before every session open, so a capture/accessibility grant made after a denial is seen without restarting the service. (#12831)

  • computer.run: a QuickJS cell with wall-clock, call and payload limits and an escape battery (no host bridge yet). (#12831)

  • macOS capture: SkyLight-first window capture with SCK/legacy fallback, capture of windows on another Space, and a measured replayd consent probe (opt-in, never gating). (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: epoch sources (display change, lock, sleep, Space switch, engine restart) announced as epoch.changed; stale frames after an epoch move answer InvalidCoordinateFrame superseded. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: the ref-generation store keeps 8 live generations per target and up to 65,536 retained objects; refs past their generation answer StaleRef. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: object-mode ax.snapshot returns a partial observation (complete, nodesObserved, cuts) at a 3000 ms default deadline and a node budget; null mode is byte-equal to v1. (#12831)

  • Desktop engine: per-stage timings on action reports and a redacted trace JSONL sink, both behind the trace session option (default off). (#12831)

  • Wayland (KWin): window-targeted pointer input through libei behind the windowInput capability probe, plus the KDE QA driver. (#12831)

  • Kernel computer globals (JS and Python preludes) are generated from the SDK facade manifest; verify lives on the desktop root, drill on window handles. (#12831)

  • Wayland (GNOME): window-targeted pointer input through a small Shell extension that publishes the window list over the session bus. (#12831)

v5.1.68 Stable2026-11-10T19:33:16Z

Release binaries may be up to 210 MiB. The 5.1.67 publish stopped on Linux x64 because its binary measured 209,889,456 bytes, 174,256 bytes over the 200 MiB per-binary budget (5.1.66's was 209,664,176). The budget moves to 210 MiB, about 5% above that size, and still fails any release that goes past it; the 5 MiB headroom warning is unchanged. (#13052) (#13052)

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v18.18.8 Stable2026-11-11T03:31:40Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Fixed

  • Fixed concurrent OAuth requests rejected as claude_code_version_too_old failing instead of retrying with the required version (#18651)
  • Chat Completions gateway requests preserve explicit tool strict-mode choices, so Codex tools can omit optional arguments when callers set strict: false (#18672 by @0xeb).

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Muse Ember (muse-ember-1.0) on Meta Model API and Muse Code with the minimal–max reasoning effort ladder and its >200K long-context pricing
  • Added Gemini 4 Carbon pricing on the Gemini API, Vertex AI, Google Antigravity, and Cursor ($2 input / $0.10 cached / $10 output per 1M tokens through 2026-11-30, then $4 / $0.20 / $20), instead of showing $0 (#18734 by @eggpeat)

Fixed

  • Fixed regenerating the catalog marking 18 reasoning models as non-reasoning when NanoGPT's <id>:thinking sibling row had larger limits (#18673, #18728)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Changed

  • curl -fsSL https://omp.sh/install | sh and install.ps1 now install the standalone binary by default, even when bun is present, so installs get delta updates; pass --source/-Source for a bun package install
  • Ask forms can attach notes to selected options; those notes accompany the answers separately from submitted form values (#18779 by @Shadorain)

Fixed

  • Claude auto-redeem now spends a full saved reset on an account blocked by its weekly limit when the reset also clears the Fable cap; such resets were treated as Fable-only and skipped while Fable usage had room (#18801)
  • Fixed agents showing as idle in the Agent Hub while still running after a failed streaming edit preview (edit.streamingAbort) auto-recovered the turn (#18790 by @H4vC)
  • A browser call on a tab that is not open now says to open it with await browser.open({ name }) (Python: await browser.open(name=…)) and the url and app it was opened with, instead of pointing at the removed action:"open" (#18739 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed computer.window({ app }) and computer.windows({ app }) matching similarly named apps when the exact app name has an open window, such as Google Chrome alongside Google Chrome for Testing (#18744 by @will-bogusz)
  • In Tern, browser role/ selectors keep input roles and names under Turkish and Azeri locales (#18658 by @will-bogusz)
  • Chrome role/RootWebArea waits with visible return the document instead of failing, and hidden waits keep waiting (#18658 by @will-bogusz)
  • An omp -p or other omp that your session's bash tool or eval cell starts inside Tern or cmux no longer picks Tern or cmux from the pane it inherited, so its browser tabs stop floating over your pane (#18773 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser relay connections sharing a tab no longer lose cross-site frames when another connection disables auto-attachment on its page; automatic children detach locally when their last armed page leaves, while explicitly attached frame subtrees remain usable after opting out (#18657 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser agents now say when a step can't be done through the page, instead of patching the page with scripts and reporting the step as done (#18761 by @will-bogusz)
  • An extension's assistant_message rewrite now reaches the next Codex Responses request: same-model native replay used to resend the captured original text while the transcript showed the rewritten one (#18312)
  • MCP reconnects and tool-list refreshes now keep extension-deactivated tools inactive while enabling newly discovered tools (#18678).
  • Exiting after aborting a bash command now waits for its TERM-resistant foreground processes to be killed (#18669).
  • An edit touching several files that anchors lines no read displayed now reveals those lines for every file in one rejection, so the identical resend applies instead of being rejected again for the next file (#18742 by @MaisonnatM)
  • omp auth-gateway stdio now serves judge, image, speech, embedding, rerank and video models: /v1/models lists them, and /v1/systemone answers typesafe/jev-* or @judge instead of 404, or 422 from a chat model (#18740 by @andyhite)
  • omp auth-gateway stdio no longer cuts its last responses off at 128 KiB when its input ends before a slow reader has read them (#18740 by @andyhite)
  • omp --session <id> --restore restores that session, as omp --restore --session <id> does, instead of opening the session picker: a bare --restore keeps a session --session or --resume already named (#18685 by @AdamDureyAtWillship)
  • The legacy Pi virtual module now builds in an npm install that keeps the bundled Pi packages nested: it is anchored to the installed package, so its imports resolve through that package's own dependency graph (#15208, #18733 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • User-level agents, commands, and other ~/.omp/agent config (including agents saved from the agents hub) now follow PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR, matching where omp agents unpack writes (#18684)
  • Fixed grep and glob treating a ; list that starts with an internal URL and includes a local path, such as artifact://1;notes.txt, as one address; each listed target is now searched (#18714 by @eggpeat)
  • Isolated tasks that edit a file whose name contains spaces now commit that file, including in a nested git repository, instead of leaving it unstaged or committing a different file that shares part of the name (#18715 by @eggpeat)
  • A disposed or switched session now releases every persistent shell it owns, including the ones a surface keeps under a name of its own (/loop conditions), instead of holding one native shell per surface for the life of the process (#18717 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/collab-web

Changed

  • Ask result cards render saved notes beneath their matching selected answers (#18779 by @Shadorain)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Changed

  • macOS keys, typing and held keys for a window now go to a sheet attached to it that holds keyboard focus, such as an Open sheet or Finder's Go to Folder, as macOS sends them, instead of throwing InvalidTarget and naming the sheet; so do keys for a control of that sheet, which reports the window under it as its own (#18675 by @will-bogusz)
  • Computer input no longer fails with InputBusy the moment another omp session's call holds the desktop, such as an apps.open waiting for its window: it waits up to 5 seconds for that call to finish, on macOS, Linux and Windows (#18746 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • Fixed a crash when parsing Markdown with very deeply nested lists or block quotes, such as a line of several hundred - markers, which made editing such files kill the process (#18707)
  • Fixed the shell's git builtin on Windows with bash.gitGuard (or smart git) on failing every allowed command with exit 127 (…\Git\cmd\git: No such file or directory); it now runs git.exe (#18722)
  • Computer element searches accept native role names, spaced role names such as text field, and radiobutton for macOS radio controls without changing printed accessibility roles (#18745 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS background input and takeover refusing every call, with nothing sent, while Finder is in front with its focus on the desktop and a Finder window open; Finder gets its front status back with its focus left on the desktop (#18680 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Changed

  • Ask forms support notes on options: n on radio/checkbox rows and Alt+n on select menus (app.ask.note), with selected notes shown alongside submitted answers and notes on unchosen options marked as drafts (#18779 by @Shadorain)

Fixed

  • Ask form textareas edited from Tern now restart the inactivity timeout instead of submitting mid-edit
  • Fixed a picture in an ask form staying painted on screen after the form closed (#18693 by @jaredlyon)
  • Fixed terminal Device Attributes replies (such as ^[[?64;1;2;4;6;17;18;21;22c) leaking into the shell prompt on exit (#18662 by @asuffield)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Removed

  • Removed getConfigAgentDirName(); use getAgentDir(), which also honors PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR (#18684)

v18.18.7 Stable2026-11-10T20:23:32Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • LiteLLM usage adapter (@oh-my-pi/pi-ai/usage/litellm) reporting a gateway's key and user USD budgets (#17873 by @maykonmichel)
  • UsageApi.replaceConfiguredProviders registers configuration-sourced usage providers between runtime overrides and the built-in resolver, and UsageProvider.usesRequestKey makes aggregate usage probe only the key inference would send (#17873 by @maykonmichel)

Changed

  • Providers with a login-warning auth rule show their account risk before a new login and start it only after the user answers yes; any other answer or a cancel stores nothing, and their login-list entry is marked "(at your own risk)" (#18499)

Fixed

  • Claude Fable launch resets now report the Fable weekly limit they clear instead of an unknown window (#18661 by @TheAifam5)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Muse Ember (muse-ember-1.0) on Meta Model API and Muse Code with the minimal–max reasoning effort ladder and its >200K long-context pricing
  • Added the login-warning "<text>" auth rule, which makes a new login accept the stated account risk first (#18499)

Changed

  • Resumed Google Antigravity behind a login warning instead of pausing it (#18499)

Fixed

  • Morph (api.morphllm.com) requests now send x-session-id, keeping a conversation on the worker that holds its prompt cache (#18704 by @wolfiesch)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • /usage and omp usage show LiteLLM key and user budgets for the built-in litellm provider and every models.yml provider with discovery.type: litellm, unless listed in disabledProviders (#17873 by @maykonmichel)
  • Extensions can ask the operator before an interactive quit, or cancel it, with session_before_shutdown; a further /exit or Ctrl+D skips the pending prompts and closes (#18424 by @aw1cks)

Changed

  • Saved-reset auto-redeem is set per window: codexResets.autoRedeem5h/autoRedeemWeekly and claudeResets.autoRedeem5h/autoRedeemWeekly/autoRedeemFable replace each provider's autoRedeem, whose value migrates into the 5h and weekly keys; a Claude Fable launch reset is governed by autoRedeemFable alone and spent only for a block that includes the Fable weekly cap, or before expiry when Fable usage is above zero (#18661 by @TheAifam5)
  • Sessions without a prompt UI (such as omp -p) no longer spend saved resets while auto-redeem is unset, even ones about to expire (#18661)
  • Google Antigravity is back in /login and omp login, marked "(at your own risk)": a new login first explains that Google may suspend accounts that sign in from third-party tools and continues only after you type yes; saved Antigravity logins work again (#18499)
  • On Windows, the bash tool's description tells the model it runs in an emulated bash environment, not cmd or PowerShell, so it writes bash instead of dir, %VAR% and other cmd syntax

Fixed

  • Extension prompts shown from session_before_* handlers no longer time out after 30 seconds while the operator is still answering (#18424 by @aw1cks)
  • A second exit request now skips pending worktree.onExit prompts and keeps those worktrees instead of waiting for an answer (#18424 by @aw1cks)
  • Advice that makes the agent keep working after it finished no longer comes back a second time, often as a blocker, while the agent is fixing it: advisor reviews of the earlier answer are cancelled and the advisor reviews the fix instead (#18735 by @H4vC)
  • On Windows, the bash tool's shell resolves MSYS drive paths (/c/Users/..., /mnt/c/...) everywhere, so running /c/.../tool.exe, cd /c/..., [ -f /c/... ] and < /c/... reach drive C: like they already did for ls and other built-in utilities
  • On Windows, cmd-style command lines such as dir | find /c /v "", sort /r, timeout /t 5 and whoami /all in the bash tool run the Windows programs they were written for, instead of pi's GNU-style find/sort/timeout/whoami (which read /c as drive C: and could walk the whole drive)
  • Changing or unsetting PATH in the bash tool's shell now forgets remembered command locations, as bash does, so a command no longer runs from a directory that has left PATH
  • One slow or flooding collab guest no longer ends sharing for the room: the host drops that guest's backlog and asks it to rejoin, sessions larger than the send budget can be joined, and guest questions nobody received settle as unavailable (#18629 by @iliaal)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Changed

  • grep and ast_grep results with no matches render as one line with 0 matches in the warning color, dropping the separate No matches found row (#18700 by @JustasMasiulis)

Fixed

  • Saved-reset messages name the Claude Fable weekly limit as "Claude Fable weekly" instead of its raw id (#18661 by @TheAifam5)

What's Changed

  • feat(extensions): add an interactive pre-shutdown hook by @aw1cks in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18424
  • fix(tui): show grep's 0 matches in yellow on one line by @JustasMasiulis in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18700
  • fix(shell): resolve MSYS drive paths (/c/...) in the engine on Windows by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18703
  • fix(catalog): pin Morph conversations to one worker with x-session-id by @wolfiesch in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18704
  • fix(shell): run cmd-syntax command lines through the Windows programs pi's utilities shadow by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18711
  • feat(coding-agent): split saved-reset auto-redeem into 5h, weekly and Claude Fable settings by @TheAifam5 in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18661
  • fix(advisor): stop superseded reviews from re-raising advice the primary is already fixing by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18735

v2.14.10v2.14.10 — ad-creative (2.12.5 → 2.13.0): ten new static ad templates from a review of long-running… Stable2026-11-11T02:26:00Z
  • ad-creative (2.12.5 → 2.13.0): ten new static ad templates from a review of long-running ads in public ad libraries: Apology Letter ("We're so sorry"), Important Update, Reassurance List ("Stop thinking something is wrong with you"), Simple Guarantee, With / Without, Venn Diagram, Stages (progression comic), Character Story ("This is Mike"), Signs Checklist, and Exaggerated Visual. Their tiers are marked provisional. A new visual styles table (dark mode, retro screen, billboard, notice board, presentation slide, whiteboard, handwritten sign, mirror writing, news card, newspaper, vintage, illustration, meme, retro app frame) restyles any template, with rules for styles that borrow a real look: no copied mastheads, no fake system dialogs or "sensitive content" screens, no tabloid claims. Health templates note Meta's personal-attributes and personal-health rules. The format map clarifies that the F-tier fake-native rating covers ads posing as a real interface, not decorative retro frames.

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 53 skills. Claude web target: 53 skills with reviewed short descriptions. Perplexity Computer target: 53 skills.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Choose <name>-perplexity.zip for Perplexity Computer (SKILL.md at the archive root). Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

  • None.

v0.2.21Sub2API 0.2.21 Stable2026-11-11T02:21:55Z

AI API Gateway Platform - 将 AI 订阅配额分发和管理

新增 Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2 支持；OpenAI 用量与计费区分 30m/12h 缓存写入 TTL，并根据实例内存上限自动设定请求体内存预算，减少高并发下误报 503 的情况。

新增功能

  • 支持 Claude Fable 5.2 与 Mythos 5.2：包括模型目录、Anthropic 预设、Bedrock 映射、OpenCode 配置和兜底定价（$8/$40 每百万 token，Fast 模式 2 倍）
  • OpenAI 缓存写入计费按 TTL 拆分：渠道、分组、账号统计价卡新增独立的 12h 缓存写入价（GPT-5.6 / GPT-6 系列未配置时默认按输入价 2.5 倍；已配置通用写入价的价卡不受影响），管理员用量详情按实际 TTL 展示
  • Antigravity 账号用量单元格新增「查询」按钮，可跳过缓存强制刷新用量
  • EasyPay 支付实例可单独配置退款成功码（默认 1，可选 0），兼容退款以 code=0 表示成功的易支付网关

优化改进

  • 请求体内存预算未配置时，自动取进程内存上限的 1/4（取物理内存、cgroup 限制、GOMEMLIMIT 中的最小值），最低 1GiB
  • API 密钥列表不再等近 30 天用量统计返回才渲染，并为用量统计新增覆盖索引，大数据量下 /keys 页面不再长时间停在骨架屏
  • 账号列表刷新时保留现有行和配额数值，不再整表闪烁成骨架屏
  • 系统设置页的保存按钮改为吸附在视口右下角的浮标，长表单不用滚到底部也能保存
  • 余额充值倍率支持最多 6 位小数，便于跟随实时汇率
  • 安全审计任务入队后立即唤醒本进程 worker 认领，不再平均等待约 250ms
  • OpenAI WebSocket 首 token 时间改为遵循管理员配置的 TTFT 口径，与 SSE 保持一致
  • 客户端未提供会话标识时，Responses 会话种子在工具回合中保持稳定，减少粘性会话中途切换账号
  • 内置 Claude Code CLI 版本基线升至 2.1.321

Bug 修复

  • 修复 OpenAI 合成的 reasoning effort 未应用 effort 映射规则和上限校验的问题；保留已配置的 GLM 降级语义
  • 修复 OpenAI 显式配置的模型级冷却规则对 overloaded/slow_down 及流内 503 不生效的问题；关键词只匹配实际错误字段
  • 修复 Codex 元数据事件（codex.rate_limits 等）提前提交响应流，导致后续上游过载无法 failover 的问题
  • 修复原生 Responses WebSocket 在后续轮次限流时重放首帧，可能导致任务上下文错乱的问题（现以 1013 关闭，由客户端重连）
  • 修复 OpenAI OAuth 池化 WebSocket 在未声明搜索的连接上发起 web_search 被上游拒绝的问题
  • 修复 Codex Desktop 注入的页面上下文导致首个 Responses 请求返回 400 的问题
  • 修复原生 Codex 生图请求因心跳提前写出 200、掩盖上游错误状态码的问题
  • 修复 /v1/responses 重建函数调用时丢失 encrypted_function_args 与 namespace 的问题
  • 修复 CC Switch 的 reasoning 封装被当作 encrypted_content 发往上游的问题
  • 修复 Anthropic 兼容路径（Chat Completions / Responses）忽略账号自定义错误策略的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 兼容入口非流式响应把思考摘要混入正文的问题
  • 修复 Antigravity 并行工具结果中的图片打断 functionResponse 序列导致上游 400 的问题
  • 修复 Antigravity Pro 账号 Argon 配额与其他 Gemini 配额共用冷却、互相误伤的问题
  • 修复账号信息加载失败时已占用的并发槽位未释放、要等 TTL 过期才恢复的问题
  • 修复刷新令牌并发轮换时同一令牌可被重复使用的问题
  • 修复定价目录哈希与内容版本不一致时，后续同步一直被跳过的问题
  • 修复 Sonnet 5.5 兜底缓存读取价格错误的问题（改为 $0.10 每百万 token）
  • 修复 OpenAI 普通输入免费时 Fast 倍率丢失的问题
  • 修复含非法 UTF-8 的 Responses 请求体被当作上游可重试错误的问题，现直接返回 400

升级指南

  • 本次新增两个数据库迁移，升级时自动执行。其中 249 在 usage_logs 上用 CONCURRENTLY 建索引，不锁表，但表较大时首次启动会耗时较长
  • 请求体内存预算的配置键是 gateway.request_body_memory_budget_bytes（v0.2.20 发布说明误写为 security.*），需要固定值时请显式设置

📥 Installation

Docker:

# Docker Hub
docker pull weishaw/sub2api:0.2.21

# GitHub Container Registry
docker pull ghcr.io/wei-shaw/sub2api:0.2.21

One-line install (Linux):

curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Wei-Shaw/sub2api/main/deploy/install.sh | sudo bash

Manual download: Download the appropriate archive for your platform from the assets below.

📚 Documentation

  • GitHub Repository
  • Installation Guide

studio-v2.60.0Reasonix Studio v2.60.0 Stable2026-11-11T02:09:16Z

本版让内置浏览器可以点选页面元素并把修改直接存回本地网页，会话之间的消息不再需要批准，工作台可并排显示文件树。

新增

  • 内置浏览器新增「点选页面元素」：点一下页面上的元素，就能看到它的选择器、文字和颜色 (6c9c6851a)
  • 从工作区本地文件打开的网页，可以直接改所选元素的文字、文字颜色、背景色和字号，并保存回文件；文件源码里对不上的元素会说明原因，不会写入 (6c9c6851a)
  • 工作台在打开的文件旁边显示文件树 (2ff95dbc5)

变更

  • 「让模型把任务交给其他会话」开关打开时（默认开启），你自己的其他会话发来的消息直接交给模型，不再出现批准卡；关闭开关则仍需批准 (4dc2b1938)
  • 保存会话时不再为每条消息复制一份数据，长会话的保存更快、占用更少内存 (e19fc12a5)

升级须知

  • 会话间消息默认不再需要批准：同机其他会话写进消息目录的内容，会在开关打开时直接进入模型；不想要请在设置的「会话间消息」里关闭派发开关 (4dc2b1938)

<!-- lang:en -->

New

  • The built-in browser can pick an element on the page: click it to see its selector, text and colors (6c9c6851a)
  • On a page opened from a workspace file, the picked element's text, text color, background and size can be changed and saved back to the file; an element the file's source cannot place is refused with the reason and nothing is written (6c9c6851a)
  • The workbench shows the file tree beside the open file (2ff95dbc5)

Changed

  • While the switch that lets the model hand tasks to other sessions is on (the default), a message from your other sessions reaches the model without an approval card; with the switch off it still waits for approval (4dc2b1938)
  • Saving a session no longer copies every message, so long sessions save faster and use less memory (e19fc12a5)

Upgrade notes

  • Messages between sessions no longer need approval by default: anything another session on this machine writes to the message directory goes straight to the model while the switch is on; turn the dispatch switch off under Messages between sessions in Settings to keep the card (4dc2b1938)

Full Changelog: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/compare/studio-v2.59.0...studio-v2.60.0

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

microsoft/terminal ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v1.26.3133.0Windows Terminal Preview v1.26.3133.0 Prerelease2026-11-11T00:12:18Z

A small servicing release for Terminal 1.26, most of which has also been backported to 1.25. Thanks everybody for using Preview and Canary!

Changes

  • You can now disable drag/drop tab management (json enableTabDragDrop, boolean, default true; Interaction page in Settings) (#20394) (thanks @dovholuknf!)
  • You can now disable the new "resize" indicator (json showResizeOverlay, boolean, default true; Interaction page in Settings) (#20861) (#20869) (thanks @nakultt!)
  • Experimental settings are now indicated by a high-visibility badge in the Settings UI (#20748) (#20775)
  • We've brought the Serbian (Cyrillic) localization up to date (#20851) (thanks @eevan78!)

Bug Fixes

Accessibility

  • Screen reader users can now interact with the "cards" in the Settings page more easily (#20871)
  • The "delete" button in the Actions editor will be read out more reasonably by screen readers (#20767)
  • The "delete" confirmation flyouts will no longer be truncated on higher Text Scaling settings (#20868)

Terminal Emulation

  • The Kitty Keyboard Protocol mode will now be maintained properly across main/alternate screen buffer switches (#20768)
  • LNM line feed mode will no longer be mishandled when it is toggled before and after a newline in the same packet (#20863)
  • Applications can no longer set window titles longer than 1000 characters (like xterm) (#20839)
  • Applications which scroll the screen buffer will once again be translated properly (such as vi from 1996) (#20774)
  • Clicking on a URL while an application is reading mouse reports will no longer generate a spurious mouse release event (#20756) (thanks @OmarMashal0!) (EDIT: sike, it looks like this is not fixed?)
  • Injected keyboard input will no longer come out with unexpected vkey values such as 0xFFFF (#20800) (thanks @shrikargs7-cloud!)
  • Terminal once again sends the ENHANCED_KEY state to console applications for Alt and F7 (#20619) (thanks @TheJesper!)

Others

  • useAcrylicInTabRow users rejoice! We've fixed one of the major sources of flickering in the title bar (#20881)
  • file: URIs containing Unicode characters will no longer send us into a wild tailspin (#20809)
  • Terminal will no longer load up a US keyboard on you (whoops) (#20769)
  • The experimental repositionCursorWithMouse setting will no longer fire in unexpected places (such as pager output) (#20852)
  • We have fixed an issue in migrating the confirmCloseAllTabs setting (#20783)
  • Writing a non-numeric value in the command history pop-up will no longer act unexpectedly (#20779)
  • You can once again search Settings for settings which are controlled by a check box (rather than a toggle switch) (#20808)

Binary files inside the unpackaged distribution archive bear the version number 1.26.261109003-preview.

View release →

v1.25.3131.0Windows Terminal v1.25.3131.0 Stable2026-11-11T00:12:16Z

With this release, Terminal 1.25 is being rolled out to General Availability; see the prior version for overall release notes.

Changes

  • (1.26 backport) conhost: we now always use the "cls" detector to prevent screen clearing from committing the entire (virtualized) text buffer (#19957)

Bug Fixes

Accessibility

  • The "delete" confirmation flyouts will no longer be truncated on higher Text Scaling settings (#20868)

Terminal Emulation

  • The Kitty Keyboard Protocol mode will now be maintained properly across main/alternate screen buffer switches (#20768)
  • LNM line feed mode will no longer be mishandled when it is toggled before and after a newline in the same packet (#20863)
  • Applications can no longer set window titles longer than 1000 characters (like xterm) (#20839)
  • Applications which scroll the screen buffer will once again be translated properly (such as vi from 1996) (#20774)
  • Clicking on a URL while an application is reading mouse reports will no longer generate a spurious mouse release event (#20756) (thanks @OmarMashal0!) (edit: er, I (@DHowett) cannot confirm that this is actually fixed)
  • Injected keyboard input will no longer come out with unexpected vkey values such as 0xFFFF (#20800) (thanks @shrikargs7-cloud!)
  • Terminal once again sends the ENHANCED_KEY state to console applications for Alt and F7 (#20619) (thanks @TheJesper!)

Others

  • useAcrylicInTabRow users rejoice! We've fixed one of the major sources of flickering in the title bar (#20881)
  • file: URIs containing Unicode characters will no longer send us into a wild tailspin (#20809)
  • Terminal will no longer load up a US keyboard on you (whoops) (#20769)
  • Writing a non-numeric value in the command history pop-up will no longer act unexpectedly (#20779)

Binary files inside the unpackaged distribution archive bear the version number 1.25.261109001.
