debpalash/VoiceStudio ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.5.8-beta.4v0.5.8-beta.4 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-10T22:52:54Z

Installer signing

Platform Signing
macOS (Apple Silicon) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS (Intel) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Windows x64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Linux x64 — Linux packages are not code-signed

Phone downloads

Android: signed APK · Play bundle.

iPhone: unsigned sideload IPA. Sign with your own Apple ID to install; this is not a TestFlight build.

AMD and Intel GPUs now speed up OmniVoice, and setup gets far more forgiving. OmniVoice GGUF runs on AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, dots.tts arrives on Windows with no Python setup, and a failing PyTorch download or a broken GPU no longer leaves you without a working app.

New since beta.3: audio.cpp v0.9.4, hardware video encoders for dub exports, the Ming-3.0-tts and MOSS-SoundEffect engines, a voicestudio terminal command, streaming OpenAI-compatible speech, Windows ROCm for RDNA 1 and 2 GPUs, per-voice pronunciation dictionaries, Convert voice for video, source-language dubbing, a lighter startup, a tidier setup screen and account page, and dozens of dubbing, setup, Snap and Android fixes.

Testing wanted: these get their first real-hardware test in this beta, so reports are welcome: the audio.cpp v0.9.4 runtime, hardware video encoders for dub exports, the Ming-3.0-tts engine, sound effects (MOSS-SoundEffect, NVIDIA GPUs only), the Snap fixes and the Windows uninstall prompt that offers to delete downloaded models. OmniVoice GGUF resident mode is opt-in and inactive until a build ships the tts-server binary.

Download

Platform Installer
Windows x64 Installer
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer
macOS Apple Silicon DMG
macOS Intel DMG
Linux x64 AppImage · deb
Android APK · AAB
iPhone Unsigned IPA (sideload with your own Apple ID)

Upgrading: Install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept.

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux
  • dots.tts voice cloning now runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery, changing only the voice
  • Dubbing exports subtitles before dubbing and moves lines between speakers
  • Setup falls back to the CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails

Changed

  • The backend starts lighter and faster: speech-recognition engines are checked in a short-lived helper process instead of being loaded at startup, while broken installs are still reported as unavailable (#3924, #3946)
  • The app opens with less code to load: the audio and video players load with the first clip played and returning users skip the first-run setup screens, cutting the startup bundle by about 6%, and a new offline benchmark with a CI check on bundle growth tracks startup, memory, CPU and bundle size (#3924, #3940, #3943)
  • Settings → VoiceStudio account lists what to sync and your devices as compact, aligned rows with icons, a Pro badge with one Get Pro link for locked items, and device rows that show OS and when each was last active (#3937)
  • The runtime setup screen fits one window: one status line, the install folder with free space, one main action (or the exact fix, such as choosing another folder when the drive is full), with language, region, Python and remote options under Options and recovery tools under More fixes (#3934)
  • OmniVoice GGUF can keep its runtime loaded between requests to skip the reload before each generation, as an opt-in under Settings → Performance; it stays inactive until a build ships omnivoice.cpp's tts-server, so nothing changes today (#3965, #3654)
  • The audio.cpp runtime updates to v0.9.4; installed Breeze-TTS-2, dots.tts and Sortformer models keep working without a re-download, and the previous runtime keeps running them until you install the update (#3129)
  • Updates now download in the background and install when you quit, with a compact title-bar pill for progress, Restart and Retry, clearer error messages and a restart prompt when work is running (#3928)
  • The top bar is more compact: the account button is just your profile photo, or a person icon when signed out, and the GitHub star count is shortened (#3922).
  • Product analytics moved from PostHog to Supermetry, still only after a yes at the first-run prompt, and now also records which engine ran each generation and transcription. (#3902)
  • Restyled Settings → VoiceStudio account to match the home page: one account card with your plan and storage, the email shown once, and a storage bar visible at 0%. (#3885)
  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)
  • Mac releases use an Apple API key for notarization instead of an account password. (#3775) — thanks @velixio!
  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout. (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)
  • The OmniVoice sidecar availability check no longer imports the model, so backend startup reconcile finishes about 6 s sooner (#3534) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Added

  • Qwen Base can deliver audio during generation on the speech WebSocket (#3992) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • OpenAI-compatible PCM speech can stream during synthesis, with native Qwen3-TTS Base support on Apple Silicon and provisioned NVIDIA systems (#3976) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • The account menu in the window corner shows your profile photo, email, plan and cloud storage, with a Manage plan link (#3955)
  • The VoiceStudio account card has a larger avatar that opens your profile page to change the photo, an inline display-name editor, a plan badge with icon, an iconized storage meter, and icon buttons for refresh and sign out (#3948)
  • Pronunciation dictionaries can now belong to a single voice: switch the pronunciation settings to This voice and its entries win over the language dictionary whenever that voice speaks (#3873)
  • New voicestudio terminal command: use voices, speech, transcription, dubbing and models from your terminal, with VoiceStudio starting in the background when needed, plus Tab completion (#3828) — thanks @mostafa-ismail-2004!
  • An OpenAI-compatible LLM provider with an API key can now use plain HTTP on a trusted LAN IP address, through a new per-provider opt-in that is off by default (#3811)
  • Ming-3.0-tts, an opt-in 0.5B voice-cloning engine for Chinese, English, Japanese, Korean, French, German and Spanish on NVIDIA GPUs, installs in its own environment from Model Catalogue (#3793)
  • Convert voice takes a video: its voice is swapped with Keep my delivery and you get the video back, keeping picture and background when they can be preserved, otherwise the converted audio with a message (#3750)
  • Dubbing can now keep the original transcript and speak it in the source language, skipping translation, from the segment menu (#3747)
  • Experimental native Windows ROCm setup for RDNA 1 gfx1010 GPUs (RX 5700 / 5700 XT) runs PyTorch TTS on the GPU, with speech recognition on the CPU (#3734) — thanks @sdf123098!

Fixed

  • Update MLX to 0.32.4 to address a shutdown crash after Apple Silicon speech generation (#3991) — thanks @kellerkind84!

  • Large dubbing stem and segment ZIP downloads no longer hold the entire archive in backend memory (#3986) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Audiobook chapters and dub segments wait for a speech engine that is briefly busy streaming instead of failing; if it stays busy, the audiobook stops with a clear error and keeps its progress for a retry (#3987)
  • Apple Silicon speech requests now retry cleanly when a native Qwen stream is using the MLX model (#3981) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • CosyVoice 3 GGUF now explains why it is unavailable (runtime, model files or device probe) and falls back to the runtime's CPU path when the Vulkan probe fails (#3980)
  • Older NVIDIA cards (GTX 900/1000 series) now get an accurate explanation and a pointer to the Vulkan engines instead of advice to install a cu128 PyTorch build (#3978)
  • Storage, setup download and wheel prefetch sizes move to the next unit instead of showing "1024 KB" or "1024 MB" (#3972) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Dubbing now tells you which speakers had too little clean speech to clone and use the default voice, and shows transcription warnings such as a missing diarization model (#3419, #3971)
  • Dubbing now retries a failed background separation on a lighter setting and then on the CPU, and tells you right away if the original background could not be kept (#3751, #3970)
  • Voice cloning decodes non-WAV references for add-on engines and keeps cached source paths relative (#3709, #3958) — thanks @samay-hash!
  • Setup now retries a download that hits a temporary server error (such as a 504) and restarts one that gets no data for two minutes, instead of failing or waiting fifteen (#3882, #3950, #3956, #3957) — thanks @velixio!
  • Android: a phone voice clone stopped by leaving the app or rotating the phone now says so and keeps the sample and name for Retry (#3953) — thanks @hkarmoush!
  • The Nix flake build no longer fails on unrelated changes: its dependency install is built from the package manifests alone and is deterministic (#3949)
  • The desktop helper no longer wakes every 8 ms while a dictation shortcut is set, so the installed app stays idle in the background (#3924, #3945)
  • Updates no longer leave a second copy of the installed update (about 270 MB) in the updater cache; a downloaded newer update is kept (#3939)
  • Sidebar tabs such as Design and Saved no longer cut off to a single letter next to the macOS window buttons (#3926)
  • MCP tools now say why VoiceStudio refused a request, such as a model licence to accept, instead of a bare HTTP status, with secrets and home paths removed (#3916) — thanks @meosbrand!

  • OmniVoice on an older NVIDIA GPU that the installed PyTorch has no kernels for now shows as running on the CPU and gets the CPU time allowance, so slow renders are not cut short (#3565, #3067)
  • When Windows Smart App Control, an application control policy or an antivirus blocks one of the engine's files, the error names the blocker and VoiceStudio no longer re-downloads the runtime (#3557, #3209, #3224) — thanks @ark2006aug13-byte and @pauloerv-sketch!
  • First-run setup downloads large wheels such as the Windows CUDA torch over several connections and resumes them after Cancel or Retry, instead of crawling at about 1 MB/s on networks that limit each connection (#3557, #3411, #3060, #3202) — thanks @justcallme-A and @hadialshaer!
  • The app no longer crashes with an "insertBefore" error when browser page translation or an extension changes the page (#3555, #3168) — thanks @wwwfelipe29-ai!
  • An engine install that needs a C++ compiler, such as CosyVoice 3, now says which build tools to install instead of blaming the network (#3553, #3423)
  • Switching Linux to the AMD ROCm build of PyTorch finishes setup instead of failing on a Pillow version conflict (#3553, #3158) — thanks @Blade-pt and @prashker!
  • Older NVIDIA GPUs that the installed PyTorch has no kernels for (such as Maxwell cards) now show as running on the CPU instead of "CUDA ready" (#3553, #3067) — thanks @BheeshamKS!

  • Script samples and placeholders in every language show literal [pause 0.5s] and [voice:NAME] markup, and uncast characters get one calm note instead of warnings (#3547)
  • Interrupted Stories and Audiobook renders show once per project on their own page, explain a disabled Resume, and can be dismissed (#3547)
  • Every workspace shows the same not-ready notice with one Set up action, and disabled buttons now say why (#3547)

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.106graphify 0.9.106 Stable2026-11-10T21:53:23Z

graphify 0.9.106

  • Fix: install requires-python is capped to >=3.10,<3.15, so uv tool install graphifyy (and pip) on a fresh machine no longer selects Python 3.15 — for which tree-sitter has no wheel — and then fails with a source build that needs a C compiler (No such file or directory: 'cc'). uv now picks a wheel-having interpreter (3.14) by default, with no --python flag and no compiler needed. The Ubuntu/Debian README line that asked for python3.12 (absent on Debian 12 and Ubuntu 22.04) is replaced with the uv installer or a plain python3/pipx line (#5134).
  • Fix: cpp a plain class/struct forward declaration folds into its unique exactly-qualified definition when one file reaches the other through unconditional exact quoted includes, and a parameterless member declaration folds into its proven out-of-line definition by matching return type and qualifiers; overloads, templates, conditional declarations and unproven includes stay separate (#5117, thanks @xRedLeader).
  • Fix: cpp a member call resolves through a scoped type alias (using Alias = ns::Type;) and through inherited fields/methods, and a forward-declared pointee type is completed at the use site; ambiguous, virtual or diamond-inherited receivers fail closed (#5118, thanks @xRedLeader).
  • Fix: cpp an out-of-line template member keeps its proven primary-template owner, and calls inside a #if-guarded initializer or argument list are recovered from byte-preserving branch views (bounded, fail-closed on malformed/over-budget input); dependent receivers stay unresolved (#5115, thanks @xRedLeader).
  • Fix: python a dotted subscript annotation (mod.MyBox[Foo]) counts its value once as the type instead of also as its own generic argument, so the false generic_arg reference no longer replaces the real parameter_type/return_type edge (#5113, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: ts/js a call through an ESM namespace import (import * as ns from './m'; ns.f()), a CommonJS whole-module require (const m = require('./m'); m.f()), or a TS import = require resolves to the real declaration; fail-closed on an unknown module, a non-exported name, or an ambiguous barrel (#5114, #5129, thanks @mohitdixit02).
  • Fix: llm a precomputed FileSlice input is preserved through extract_corpus_parallel instead of crashing or stamping a partial parent complete; a slice that covers only part of its file marks the parent partial for re-dispatch (#5106, #5128, thanks @aadilrizwan).
  • Fix: query BFS walks levels in a fixed, hash-independent order, so a query's edge lines (and which survive the token budget) no longer vary with PYTHONHASHSEED (#5109, #5112, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Feat: query --no-doc-demotion (and the demote_docs rc key / MCP arg) disables the source-over-docs soft weight for a docs-first corpus; code corpora are unaffected by default (#5084, #5116, thanks @mohitdixit02).
  • Perf: resolution _collision_aware indexes salted sibling ids once instead of rescanning all nodes per use, cutting resolution time on large collision-heavy corpora (graph output byte-identical) (#5108, #5111, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: manifest a pom.xml using a prefixed, single-quoted or spaced xmlns keeps its Maven package and dependency nodes instead of losing them; a foreign namespace is not double-counted (#5126, thanks @sdivyanshu90).
  • Fix: merge-graphs graphify merge-graphs reports how many external nodes were unified across repos instead of silently absorbing them; the merge result is unchanged (#5127, thanks @igorpugatschev).
  • Fix: diagnostics diagnose_extraction no longer crashes on a malformed nodes container (null or scalar); the malformed extraction is still surfaced through the build error (#5125, thanks @sdivyanshu90).
  • Fix: html the interactive graph no longer draws a hyperedge region over members hidden by a community toggle; a region vanishes once fewer than two of its members stay visible (#5122, thanks @Yi-111-a).

The AST cache schema is bumped to 43; warm caches re-run the affected Python and C++ extractions.

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.2561.4.256 Stable2026-11-10T21:31:53Z

Smoother performance, easier dictation, saved phone drafts & clearer workspace controls ✨

Long chats stay more responsive while agents stream tool output, reasoning and subagent work, with less background processing during terminal and tab updates. This update also makes dictation easier to set up and use and makes agents and cloud workspaces easier to manage. Phone-specific improvements below require the matching phone app update.

  • 🎙️ Dictate with less setup: Voice settings suggests an on-device model for your language and offers one setup button. Add personal or repository vocabulary, remove filler words, and use Auto mode to hold the shortcut or tap to toggle. On-device dictation inserts phrases as you pause, and you can dictate into fields in Orca's browser.
  • 📱 Pick up where you left off: Chats show their saved recent messages after a phone relaunch while reconnecting. Commit messages, PR descriptions, comments, review notes, tasks and new-workspace drafts survive closing the app.
  • ⌨️ More control from your phone: The terminal key row gains Shift, Ctrl and Alt latches and an optional Smart rail with agent actions. A floating dictation pill lets you keep speaking while moving to another screen in the app.
  • 🤖 Get agents ready from chat: Supported missing agents offer installation and sign-in guidance where you start a chat. OpenCode subagents appear in their own cards and keep their chat active while they work; Codex replies can be forked after a rewind.
  • 🧭 Find the controls you need: The browser groups its tools into an Annotate button and one menu. Settings is easier to scan, and recipe-backed VM workspaces show their lifecycle with Suspend and Resume actions.

Highlights

  • Voice & dictation: Speech-model updates are offered in the dictation pill when you start dictating, with download progress, cancellation and retry. Language-based setup suggestions, model quality information, a Use GPU switch, personal and repository vocabulary, and support for OpenAI-compatible transcription services. The dictation pill shows committed and pending words; failed recordings can be retried. Pressing Send or Enter while dictating waits for the words before sending.
  • Phone & recovery: Saved chat messages and draft text return after a relaunch. Home opens with last-known workspaces, visited terminals stay mounted for quicker switching, and page assets load on demand. Folder workspaces have Source Control on the phone, and large-file diffs retain unchanged lines.
  • Chat & agents: Copy and other reply actions stay visible on the latest response, while older replies reveal them on hover or keyboard focus. Account usage shows Used, Left and time until reset. Claude chat names use the CLI's titles, questions can show option previews, Codex failures give actionable advice, and a New divider is available in Settings. Usage-limited chats that are due can continue in one pass, with automatic retries stopping after five consecutive failures.
  • Workspaces & remote hosts: Setup-script import understands more existing repository configurations. VM workspaces show creation progress and failures, and suspended VMs can be resumed from their workspace controls. Paired-server connection failures are detected when the host stops answering.
  • Browser, editor & everyday fixes: Browser annotation, drawing and element capture share one control, with zoom and viewport state in the address field. Inline or side-by-side diff choice is saved. Linear search and issue lists include the project, and agent speed menus offer the speeds supported by the selected model.

Dictation upgrade note: Existing local speech-model downloads may need an update before dictation works again. Complete the download from the dictation pill or Voice settings before relying on dictation offline.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/stablyai/orca/compare/v1.4.255...v1.4.256

openclaw/openclaw ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2026.9.37openclaw 2026.9.37 Stable2026-11-10T20:42:54Z

This is a gateway-only extended-stable release, published from a retired maintenance line by explicit maintainer decision. This release is OpenClaw from the end of September 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is 2026.11.2

2026.9.37

Highlights

  • Private inspection stays private: paired-node photos and screenshots are no longer automatically attached to replies, and explicit media-read denials are honored when preparing reply attachments. (#174814, #184964) Thanks @eleqtrizit.
  • Responsive replies and pages: long zero-width Telegram table cells and malformed plugin SVG transforms no longer stall the Gateway or browser page. (#183217, #182674) Thanks @LeizhenZhang, @obviyus, and @SebTardif Authored by @steipete. Thanks @LeizhenZhang, @obviyus, and @SebTardif.
  • Linked accounts survive resets: sessions keep their person-linked model account through /new, /reset, and idle or daily rollover. Fixes #179432. (#179433) Thanks @masatohoshino Authored by @steipete. Thanks @masatohoshino.
  • Safer update recovery: interrupted snapshot writes preserve the previous rollback point, and profile-backed updates and Doctor maintenance no longer trip over their own maintenance ownership. (#189946, #177792, #187104) Thanks @kaguranananana, @GrahamMcBain, and @RomneyDa Authored by @steipete. Thanks @kaguranananana, @GrahamMcBain, and @RomneyDa.
  • Connections recover cleanly: channel accounts can recover after a manual stop times out, and ClickClack reconnects after silent network drops. Fixes #184852. (#177444, #184853) Thanks @masatohoshino, @YangManBOBO, and @obviyus.
  • Windows state access works across path aliases: equivalent Windows paths preserve the same state ownership instead of falsely reporting offline maintenance. Fixes #176163. (#176174) Thanks @tjarrau-dev Authored by @steipete. Thanks @tjarrau-dev.

Changes

  • Faster plugin admission: reuse native-reference validation within the same admission capture instead of repeatedly walking companion directories, while revalidating replacement captures and recovery copies. Fixes #162047. (#162254) Thanks @WG-Mojo Authored by @steipete. Thanks @WG-Mojo.

Fixes

  • Private diagnostics: omit suppressed reply text and background command contents from verbose diagnostics while retaining status metadata. Thanks @steipete.
  • Media boundaries: keep paired-node inspection photos and screenshots private by default, and honor explicit read denials for reply attachments without blocking authorized managed outputs. (#174814, #184964) Thanks @eleqtrizit.
  • Restrictive group policies: Doctor preserves explicitly empty group allowlists, inherited settings, and disabled group policies instead of populating fallback senders. Fixes #173826. (#174787) Thanks @lockhartheavyindustries Authored by @steipete. Thanks @lockhartheavyindustries.
  • Discovery output: escape terminal control characters in untrusted Gateway discovery fields. (#183355) Thanks @Alix-007.
  • State and write outcomes: recognize committed SQLite writes whose worker result cannot be serialized, avoiding unsafe replay, and preserve Windows ownership across equivalent path spellings. Fixes #176163. (#177321, #176174) Thanks @tjarrau-dev Authored by @steipete. Thanks @tjarrau-dev.
  • Session accounts: retain linked model-account selections and their provenance through explicit resets and automatic rollover. Fixes #179432. (#179433) Thanks @masatohoshino Authored by @steipete. Thanks @masatohoshino.
  • Channel recovery: recover channel accounts after timed-out manual stops and restore ClickClack message reception after silent network drops. Fixes #184852. (#177444, #184853) Thanks @masatohoshino, @YangManBOBO, and @obviyus.
  • Local tool connections: replace unexpectedly closed MCP loopback listeners and retain their cleanup work for shutdown rather than leaving later tool calls permanently disconnected. Fixes #139340. (#183352) Thanks @SC-10749 and @eli-pavlov Authored by @steipete. Thanks @eli-pavlov and @SC-10749.
  • Telegram and Control UI: bound the processing of long zero-width table cells and malformed SVG transform strings without changing valid table or icon rendering. (#183217, #182674) Thanks @LeizhenZhang, @obviyus, and @SebTardif Authored by @steipete. Thanks @LeizhenZhang, @obviyus, and @SebTardif.
  • Command results and temporary files: preserve a completed command's exit result when detached-descendant cleanup is uncertain, retain a visible cleanup warning, and remove temporary MCP configuration directories when writing fails. Fixes #179555. (#185636, #179603) Thanks @xiaopan-xydt and @Yolo521-bot Authored by @steipete. Thanks @Yolo521-bot and @xiaopan-xydt.
  • Gateway credentials: keep local password SecretRefs active when a separate remote token is configured, and retain a saved node TLS pin when the fingerprint option is empty or whitespace-only. (#128175, #125646) Thanks @TheAngryPit, @CassieMei, @obviyus, @RomneyDa, and @wanyongstar.
  • Update and restart reliability: preserve rollback snapshots after interrupted writes, release completed maintenance before reopening requester policy, confirm predecessor stop receipts during Doctor maintenance, and prepare configuration restarts outside the restart-signal fence. (#189946, #177792, #187104, #172777) Thanks @kaguranananana, @GrahamMcBain, and @RomneyDa Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @kaguranananana, and @GrahamMcBain.
  • Memory jobs and imports: leave stopped or failure-disabled dreaming jobs disabled, and refresh imported ChatGPT transcript examples without overwriting real Notes sections. Fixes #180655, #193161. (#180694, #193173) Thanks @Yun-0000, @lisandromachado, @KhanCold, and @obviyus Authored by @steipete. Thanks @lisandromachado, @KhanCold, @Yun-0000, and @obviyus.
  • Release artifact verification: qualify the candidate's exact plugin artifacts, route validation through the admitted verifier, and accept successful admission jobs that omit step details while preserving run, attempt, artifact, and authority checks. (#164464, #173437, #188597) Thanks @RomneyDa.

v2026.11.3openclaw 2026.11.3 Stable2026-11-10T18:53:07Z

2026.11.3

Highlights

  • Private task results: Keep private subagent completions out of streamed chat replies. (#188490) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
  • Reliable child-task handoffs: Allow a parent to hand off to replacement work after cancelling a child, without leaving the cancelled child's completion receipt blocking the handoff. (#178893; related #178565) Thanks @brianmcguire and @letsc4.
  • Visible tool failures: Report unanswered tool failures instead of silently ending a user-addressed turn as a success. (#182458; related #99645) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @rdleclerc.
  • Bounded authentication: Stop stalled provider authentication at the run deadline and propagate cancellation through authentication work. (#189144) Thanks @amittell.
  • Sandboxed image access: Keep required-sandbox image previews inside allowed folders, even in Full Access mode, while preserving explicit administrator image allowances. (#176203) Thanks @shakkernerd.
  • Verified workspace connections: Preserve saved node certificate pins when an empty fingerprint is supplied, and verify workspace-transfer certificates before sending credentials or data. (#125646, #190002) Thanks @wanyongstar and @avelev99.
  • Faster, responsive Gateway: Reduce startup module-file loading overhead and avoid stalls when abort-command detection encounters long punctuation runs. (#192217, #184433) Thanks @karkarl, @RomneyDa, @LeizhenZhang, and @obviyus.

Changes

No new configuration options or features.

Fixes

  • Credential isolation: Keep reused and cached plugin results scoped to their connection credentials, and keep other plugins' transport hooks on the provider's origin. (#178444, #177974, #189423) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @obviyus.
  • Search and memory credentials: Keep Firecrawl search and scrape keys with their scoped endpoints and embedding credentials with their destination. (#190706, #184966) Thanks @zhangguiping-xydt and @eleqtrizit.
  • Credential redaction: Redact inline environment credentials from configuration reads and reflected search credentials from Exa errors. (#190618, #178745) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @yetval.
  • Child-process environments: Prevent managed Signal daemons and bundled language servers from inheriting unrelated Gateway secrets. (#179198, #179415) Thanks @VACInc.
  • Authentication errors: Surface credential-read failures rather than treating them as missing credentials, and reject explicitly selected auth profiles belonging to incompatible providers. (#173970, #181597) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @vincentkoc.
  • Channel safety: Keep incoming Twitch IRC content out of parser error logs and require explicit request IDs when multiple Tlon approvals are pending. (#174552, #186243) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @mmaps.
  • Scheduled jobs: Handle queued cron storage failures without exiting the Gateway, and clear one-shot cleanup when a job becomes recurring. (#175678, #168985) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @yxshee, @wangmiao0668000666, and @obviyus.
  • Updates: Let profile-backed updates recognize their own maintenance lock and preserve cancellation when ledger writes are deferred. (#177792, #187998) Thanks @GrahamMcBain and @vincentkoc.
  • Update recovery: Recover safely when the initial package-presence check times out, requiring a fresh valid directory identity before replacement and retaining rollback to the original package. (#181906) Authored by @steipete.
  • Anthropic usage caps: Recognize exhausted plural usage-limit errors without spending the transient rate-limit retry budget. (#176186; related #176179) Thanks @sunlit-deng and @blue-dot-1.
  • Terminal sessions: Forward /reset soft to the Gateway instead of hard-resetting the session. (#172431) Thanks @yetval and @obviyus.
  • Git recovery: Preserve uncertain command failures during Git repair rather than treating an unconfirmed operation as successful. (#176006) Authored by @steipete.
  • Memory provider selection: Accept mixed-case none and auto provider values. (#186310) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @sagealice2002-dotcom.

First-time preview setup

Existing stable versions do not support --channel. Download the archive for your OS and architecture from this release, verify it against SHA256SUMS, and extract the entire archive into its own directory (keep all Linux companion files together). Run the extracted binary explicitly, using its full path, rather than the old stable jcode on PATH. For Windows, use the extracted .exe.

From that preview binary, run jcode update --channel preview (substitute its full path for jcode) to persist the opt-in channel and receive future previews. To roll back, run the preview binary with update --channel stable, or reinstall the current stable release. Stable users are never moved to previews automatically.

This preview series ends when the v1.0.0 stable tag is created.

First-time preview setup

Existing stable versions do not support --channel. Download the archive for your OS and architecture from this release, verify it against SHA256SUMS, and extract the entire archive into its own directory (keep all Linux companion files together). Run the extracted binary explicitly, using its full path, rather than the old stable jcode on PATH. For Windows, use the extracted .exe.

From that preview binary, run jcode update --channel preview (substitute its full path for jcode) to persist the opt-in channel and receive future previews. To roll back, run the preview binary with update --channel stable, or reinstall the current stable release. Stable users are never moved to previews automatically.

This preview series ends when the v1.0.0 stable tag is created.

<!-- jcode-discord-announced:v1.0.0-preview.394 -->

What's new

OpenWork v0.18.66 desktop release.

  • Public artifacts use the openwork-* naming convention.
  • Cloud artifacts use openwork-cloud-* and require sign-in without enterprise activation.
  • Enterprise artifacts use openwork-enterprise-* while retaining the standard OpenWork app identity.
  • Public, Cloud, and enterprise Electron builds use separate updater manifests on this release.

Windows installers are signed using Microsoft Artifact Signing.

career-ops-v1.40.0career-ops v1.40.0: No Silent Drops Stable2026-11-10T18:29:03Z

<!-- co:release-notes:career-ops-v1.40.0 --> This release is about saying what career-ops did not read or did not keep. A board it read only in part is logged partial instead of reachable, a template tag the PDF renderer drops gets a warning, and a CV bullet whose verb drifted from the bullet you wrote is blocked.

Highlights

  • A board read in part says so. A board that career-ops could read only in part is now logged partial, not reachable (#6636), and boards that failed on a temporary error get one more try after the sweep (#6675), both by @kellymmcgrath. Before the headed-browser retry, the liveness check reads the site's robots.txt first (#6551 by @benbalter).
  • Your CV, held to your own facts. A generated bullet whose verb drifted from its source bullet is blocked (#6630 by @kellymmcgrath), structured facts are bound to the records they came from (#6016 by @luochen211), and a build with an unresolved {{token}} fails instead of printing it (#6408 by @sogav21). cv-templates.mjs lint cv <name> --render warns about what the renderer drops in silence, such as scripts and remote fonts (#5943 by @rubicon).
  • Bring your tracker with you. import-tracker.mjs imports the CSV export of another tracker (#6170), and the scan keeps a posting's close date and shows it in the pipeline (#6597), both by @mbrow190.
  • One ask at a time. career-ops asks for a GitHub star once, after your first report, and for the manifesto later, never in the same run. The asks that were spread across scan, doctor and update now live in one place (#6700).
  • Twelve more places to look. HiBob, HireHive and ALDI Nord (#6502, #6503, #6529 by @ilyakanevskiy), every Workable tenant through Workable's public XML feed (#6793 by @ilyakanevskiy), Polymer and Trakstar Hire (#6128, #6131 by @mbrow190), Tes (#6731 by @AdilAzhariOmsan), TurboHire (#6712 by @okeken), any employer's job sitemap (#6631 by @kellymmcgrath), Remote's own ATS (#6392 by @artemtrofymenko), ADP Recruiting Management (#6678 by @dgitman) and SEEK NZ (#6488 by @prateek942).

Updating

Run node update-system.mjs apply --confirm in your career-ops folder. If you said no to the update prompt earlier, this is the same command.

Thank you

xpzouying/xiaohongshu-mcp ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.7.4Release v2.7.4 Stable2026-11-10T17:46:52Z

🚀 新版本发布: v2.7.4

📋 更新内容

  • Merge pull request #912 from xpzouying/all-contributors/add-kalivid (bb6fa2e)
  • docs: update .all-contributorsrc [skip ci] (6ea38f3)

  • docs: 统一 Agent 开发规范并明确测试提交要求 (#911) (2e91b44)
  • feat: support @ mentioning users in image publishing (at_users) (20d6b69)

📦 下载说明

主程序（MCP 服务）：

  • macOS Apple Silicon: xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64
  • Windows x64: xiaohongshu-mcp-windows-amd64.exe
  • Linux x64: xiaohongshu-mcp-linux-amd64

登录工具：

  • macOS Apple Silicon: xiaohongshu-login-darwin-arm64
  • Windows x64: xiaohongshu-login-windows-amd64.exe
  • Linux x64: xiaohongshu-login-linux-amd64

🔧 使用方法

# 1. 首先运行登录工具获取 cookie
./xiaohongshu-login-darwin-arm64

# 2. 然后启动 MCP 服务
./xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64

# 或指定参数
./xiaohongshu-mcp-darwin-arm64 -headless=false

⚠️ 注意事项

  • 首次运行时会自动下载无头浏览器（约 150MB），请确保网络连接正常
  • 后续运行无需重复下载浏览器
  • 登录工具生成的 cookie 保存在 ~/.xiaohongshu/cookies.json

📊 构建信息

  • Commit: bb6fa2e208719ee634fec37881be29d3aa4699b3
  • Go Version: 1.27.3
  • Build Time: 2026-11-10T17:05:32Z

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.29CC Switch v4.0.29 Stable2026-11-10T15:29:53Z

CC Switch v4.0.29

本版本新增受管应用 GitHub Copilot CLI：自带 Key 的供应商会写进 Copilot 自己的 providers.json，在 copilot 里用 /model 就能和官方模型一起选，不用经过 CC Switch。界面新增韩语和俄语，七种语言从此同步维护，不再有缺的地方显示英文。

另外修复了 Windows 上 Codex 桌面版在 WSL 里运行 Codex 时，用第三方供应商启动就报错；Codex 新对话经路由连第三方原生 Responses 上游报 400；Codex 经路由连 Anthropic 格式上游时，长回答被 8192 的输出上限截断；Codex 官方卡在路由模式下拿不到账号自己的模型列表等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

GitHub Copilot CLI

GitHub Copilot CLI（copilot）成为新的受管应用（#8753，需求见 #5768）。供应商卡片分两种：

  • 官方登录：用 Copilot CLI 自己的 GitHub 登录，不写任何文件。「打开终端」以卡片上的模型和推理强度启动 copilot；卡片绑定了授权中心的 GitHub 账号时，用这个账号启动。卡片上显示该账号的 Copilot 额度。
  • 自带 Key 的供应商：点「添加」后写进 Copilot 自己的 providers.json（$COPILOT_HOME/providers.json）。之后在本机任何地方启动 copilot，都能在 /model 里和官方模型并列选到「卡片标识/模型」，请求直接发给供应商，不经过 CC Switch 的路由。不登录 GitHub 也能用。
    • CC Switch 只改这一家自己的条目，文件里的其他条目（Copilot 自己也会写这个文件）和顺序原样保留。providers.json 写坏了 Copilot 会起不来，所以写入前会按 Copilot 的规则把整份文件校验一遍，例如模型的最大输出不能占满整个上下文窗口。
    • Copilot 当前默认模型所在的那一家标为默认，不能移除或删除。要换，先在 copilot 里用 /model 换成别家的模型。
    • 卡片上的终端按钮要添加之后才能用，启动时直接选中这张卡的第一个模型。
    • 预设沿用 Pi 的供应商预设。

此外还支持：

  • MCP、Skills、提示词：同步到 Copilot 的 mcp-config.json、Skills 目录和指令文件。
  • 会话管理：列出 Copilot 会话，可以在终端里恢复。
  • 用量统计：从 Copilot 的会话日志导入用量；自带 Key 的供应商按会话里选中的卡片记账。
  • 一键导入链接：可以导入 Copilot 供应商，认得 providers.json 写法，支持 bearerToken，链接里的模型 ID 全部保留。

韩语、俄语界面，七种语言同步维护

  • 韩语（#8794，需求见 #2704）和俄语（#7788，需求见 #6010）：首次启动按系统语言自动选择，托盘菜单和快捷面板也已翻译。用量统计的币种新增韩元（KRW），支持自动获取汇率。
  • 土耳其语、韩语、俄语不再是「社区翻译、缺的地方显示英文」：界面上的每一句话，中文简体、中文繁体、English、日本語、한국어、Türkçe、Русский 七种语言都有，之后新增和修改文案也会七种语言一起改。
  • 升级后弹出的更新摘要暂时只有中文简体、中文繁体、英文和日文，其他语言显示英文。

变更

  • Copilot 获取模型列表为空时说明原因（#5500）：Copilot Free 账号能拿到模型，但没有一个可以在模型选择器里用，以前只提示没有找到模型。现在会提示这个账号可能没有开通 Copilot Pro；Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop 的表单还会提示可以手动填写 gpt-4.1 或 gpt-4o。账号有模型、但都不支持当前选择的上游格式时，Codex 表单会单独说明。

修复

  • Windows 上 Codex 桌面版在 WSL 里运行 Codex 时，用第三方供应商一启动就报「No such file or directory (os error 2)」（#9375）。v4.0.27 起，CC Switch 在 config.toml 里把模型目录文件写成 Windows 绝对路径，WSL 里的 Codex 读不懂这个路径，直连、路由、聚合三种模式都受影响。现在在 Windows 上、Codex 桌面版开启了在 WSL 中运行 Codex 时，改回只写文件名，下次写入时自动改正。
  • Codex 新对话经路由连第三方原生 Responses 上游时报 400（#9328，相关 #7074、#9379）。新版 Codex 桌面版会在新对话里带上没有 call_id 的页面上下文工具结果，自动化和跨任务派发的回执也是这样，校验严格的上游会拒绝整个请求。以前只对 xAI 改写，现在对所有非官方的原生 Responses 上游都改写成一条用户消息，内容保留。官方订阅和带 call_id 的工具结果不受影响。
  • Codex 经路由连 Anthropic 格式上游时，长回答被截断，报「Incomplete response returned, reason: max_output_tokens」，重试也一样。Codex 请求不带输出上限，以前按 8192 发出，思考也算在内，一次写一整个文件的工具调用就会用完。现在默认改为 32000。上游的输出上限更低时会报 400，可以在供应商的「最大输出 tokens」里填它支持的值。
  • Codex 官方卡在路由模式下拿不到账号自己的模型列表：Codex 只能用它自带的列表，缺少账号专属的模型；Codex 桌面版打开 Daybreak 后，官方模型全部变灰。现在路由正在用官方卡、且没有打开「暂停使用官方登录」时，路由会把官方的模型列表原样交给 Codex。第三方供应商不转发。
  • Claude Code 经路由连 Mistral 上的 DeepSeek 模型时，工具调用之后的每一轮都报 422（#9385、#9388）。
  • 只能经路由使用的 Claude Code 卡片，点「打开终端」后 Claude Code 每句话都提示未登录（「Not logged in」），请求根本没发出去。GitHub Copilot、Codex OAuth、xAI OAuth 这类卡片的凭据由路由提供，非 Anthropic 格式的卡片要靠路由转换格式，终端直连卡片地址用不了。现在这类卡片的「打开终端」按钮变灰，并说明要开启路由后在终端里直接运行客户端。Codex 页面上这类卡片也从隐藏按钮改为同样变灰。
  • Claude 官方卡显示的额度可能是另一个账号的：本机默认登录换成别的账号后，正在用的那张卡会读到默认登录那个账号的额度。现在会先确认默认登录属于这个账号。
  • Antigravity 的额度和 agy /usage 对不上；Google AI Ultra 5x 和 20x 都只显示「Google AI Ultra」（#9317、#9319）。
